    13	export interface ArmOutcomeFe {
    14	  id: string;
    15	  /** Receiver-labelled B at T2 per replicate, and their sum Y. */
    16	  ys: number[];
    17	  y: number;
    18	  /** Replicates in which the receiver B at T2 was above zero. */
    19	  survivedReps: number;
    20	  /** Secondary, never gating: area under the receiver-B census curve summed over replicates (each curve: sum over the censuses after the start). */
    21	  auc: number;
    22	  builderB: number;
    23	  synthesis: number | null;
    24	  saturated: boolean;
    25	  growthAttributedReps: number;
    26	  energyEnd: string;
    27	  endHashes: string[];
    28	}
    29	
    30	export const seedFor = (seed: number, k: number, p: { replicateStride: number } = PROTOCOL_FE): number => seed + k * p.replicateStride;
    31	
    32	/** Run the same placed arm for R replicate sim seeds; every census of every replicate is audited to an exact zero residual (runArm throws otherwise). */
    33	export function runArmReplicated(x: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, R: number = p.R, hooks: Hooks = {}, offsets?: readonly number[]): ArmOutcomeFe {
    34	  const ks = offsets ?? Array.from({ length: R }, (_, k) => k);
    35	  const outs: ArmOutcome[] = ks.map((k) => {
    36	    const rep: PreArm = { id: x.id, state: cloneState(x.state, { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(seed, k, p) }), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit), records: [] };
    37	    return runArm(rep, p, hooks);
    38	  });
    39	  const sum = (f: (o: ArmOutcome) => number) => outs.reduce((t, o) => t + f(o), 0);
    40	  return {
    41	    id: x.id,
    42	    ys: outs.map((o) => o.y),
    43	    y: sum((o) => o.y),
    44	    survivedReps: outs.filter((o) => o.survived).length,
    45	    auc: sum((o) => o.receiverB.slice(1).reduce((t, v) => t + v, 0)),
    46	    builderB: sum((o) => o.builderB),
    47	    synthesis: x.id === "a" ? null : sum((o) => o.synthesis ?? 0),
    48	    saturated: outs.some((o) => o.saturated),
    49	    growthAttributedReps: outs.filter((o) => o.growthAttributed).length,
    50	    energyEnd: outs[0].energyEnd,
    51	    endHashes: outs.map((o) => o.endHash),
    52	  };
    53	}

    55	export interface ContrastsFe {
    56	  builder: number;
    57	  poly: number;
    58	  space: number;
    59	  /** Free-energy-matched: Y(b) - max over every constructed comparator (d1E, d1B, fU, fS, fB). */
    60	  inoc2: number;
    61	  /** First rung's contrast, reported next to inoc2 (summed-R units). */
    62	  inoc: number;
    63	  spaceP: number;
    64	  spaceNP: number;
    65	  naive: number;
    66	  /** Paired survival (replicates with receiver present): b minus the best comparator. Attribution only. */
    67	  survivalVsInoc2: number;
    68	}
    69	
    70	export function contrastsOfFe(o: Partial<Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>>): ContrastsFe {
    71	  const g = (id: string): ArmOutcomeFe => {
    72	    const v = o[id];
    73	    if (!v) throw new NicheInvalid(`arm ${id} missing`);
    74	    return v;
    75	  };
    76	  const yb = g("b").y;
    77	  const comps = ["d1E", "d1B", "fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => o[id]);
    78	  return {
    79	    builder: yb - g("nb").y,
    80	    poly: yb - g("ab").y,
    81	    space: yb - g("c").y,
    82	    inoc2: yb - Math.max(...comps.map((id) => g(id).y)),
    83	    inoc: yb - Math.max(g("d1E").y, g("d1B").y),
    84	    spaceP: yb - g("cP").y,
    85	    spaceNP: yb - g("cNP").y,
    86	    naive: yb - g("d0").y,
    87	    survivalVsInoc2: g("b").survivedReps - Math.max(...comps.map((id) => g(id).survivedReps)),
    88	  };
    89	}

    99	export const liteTransfers = (rs: readonly TransferRecord[]): TransferLite[] => rs.map((r) => ({ op: r.op, energyBefore: r.before.energy, energyAfter: r.after.energy, residual: r.residual, matterResidual: r.matterResidual, detail: r.detail }));

   101	export interface BlockResultFe {
   102	  seed: number;
   103	  cluster: number;
   104	  valid: boolean;
   105	  invalidReason: string | null;
   106	  nPlaced: number;
   107	  dE: number;
   108	  dB: number;
   109	  dBFree: number;
   110	  refused: { id: string; reason: string }[];
   111	  costs: HistoryCost[];
   112	  /** Free energy and total energy per arm, before and after the shared inoculum placement. */
   113	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
   114	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
   115	  startTotals: Record<string, Snap>;
   116	  transfers: Record<string, TransferLite[]>;
   117	  outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>;
   118	  contrasts: ContrastsFe | null;
   119	  /** Receiver survived (B > 0) in at least R - 1 of R replicates in the field arm b. */
   120	  viable: boolean;
   121	}
   122	
   123	/** One block: Stage 1 histories (shared), transfers, shared placement, free-energy-matched comparators, R replicates of every Stage 2 arm. An invalid block is reported, never dropped. */
   124	export function runBlockFe(cluster: number, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, hooks: Hooks = {}): BlockResultFe {
   125	  const base: BlockResultFe = { seed, cluster, valid: false, invalidReason: null, nPlaced: 0, dE: 0, dB: 0, dBFree: 0, refused: [], costs: [], freeBefore: [], freeAfter: [], startTotals: {}, transfers: {}, outcomes: {}, contrasts: null, viable: false };
   126	  let arms: PreArm[] = [];
   127	  try {
   128	    const built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p);
   129	    arms = built.arms;
   130	    base.dE = built.dE;
   131	    base.dB = built.dB;
   132	    base.costs = [historyCost("builder", built.histories.builder.state, p), historyCost("nonbuilder", built.histories.nonbuilder.state, p)];
   133	    const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!, p);
   134	    base.refused = fm.refused;
   135	    base.dBFree = fm.dB;
   136	    if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid(`every free-energy-matched comparator was refused: ${fm.refused.map((r) => `${r.id} (${r.reason})`).join("; ")}`);
   137	    arms = [...arms, ...fm.arms];
   138	    base.freeBefore = freeRows(arms);
   139	    base.nPlaced = placeAll(arms, p).length;
   140	    base.freeAfter = freeRows(arms);
   141	    for (const x of arms) {
   142	      base.startTotals[x.id] = snap(x.state, x.audit);
   143	      base.transfers[x.id] = liteTransfers(x.records);
   144	    }
   145	    for (const x of arms) base.outcomes[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, p, p.R, hooks);
   146	    base.contrasts = contrastsOfFe(base.outcomes);
   147	    base.viable = base.outcomes.b.survivedReps >= p.R - 1;
   148	    base.valid = true;
   149	  } catch (e) {
   150	    if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   151	    base.invalidReason = e.reason;
   152	    for (const x of arms) base.transfers[x.id] ??= liteTransfers(x.records);
   153	  }
   154	  return base;
   155	}
   156	
   157	/** Replicate-noise control for one block: the builder-removed (b) and nonbuilder-removed (nb) arms, Y over replicate set A = offsets 0..R-1 against a disjoint set B = offsets R..2R-1. */
   158	export function noiseBlock(cluster: number, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): { absBuilder: number; absNonbuilder: number } {
   159	  if (2 * p.R - 1 > p.maxOffsetIndex) throw new NicheInvalid(`R = ${p.R} needs offsets above the reserved ${p.maxOffsetIndex}; extend the registry reservation before escalating`);
   160	  const built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p);
   161	  placeAll(built.arms, p);
   162	  const A = Array.from({ length: p.R }, (_, k) => k), B = Array.from({ length: p.R }, (_, k) => k + p.R);
   163	  const y = (id: string, offs: number[]) => runArmReplicated(built.arms.find((x) => x.id === id)!, seed, p, p.R, {}, offs).y;
   164	  return { absBuilder: Math.abs(y("b", A) - y("b", B)), absNonbuilder: Math.abs(y("nb", A) - y("nb", B)) };
   165	}
     1	// I06b free-energy-matched comparators (PLAN section 4.1). Each comparator is a separately constructed receiver-alone lattice
     2	// whose free energy E (the lattice channel) equals that of the builder-removed arm `b`, with `b`'s matter and reserve. Its ledger
     3	// starts at its own constructed total and conserves exactly: no credit enters the audit. The comparison-budget difference
     4	// constructionGap = total(b) - total(f) is signed, a BigInt, and lives in evidence only.
     5	import { CH, MOT_ZERO, allocState, cellCount, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     6	import { outsideRegion, regionCells, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     7	import { PROTOCOL_FE, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
     8	import { NicheInvalid, assertAudit, debitEven, reserveEnergy, type Audit } from "./transfer.ts";
     9	
    10	export const FREE_IDS = ["fU", "fS", "fB"] as const;
    11	export type FreeId = (typeof FREE_IDS)[number];
    12	
    13	/** Sum of the lattice free-energy channel E. */
    14	export const latticeE = (s: WorldState): bigint => totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).E;
    15	/** Total energy of an arm: lattice (all channels at their chemical value) plus the off-lattice reserve. */
    16	export const totalEnergyOf = (s: WorldState, a: Audit): bigint => totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).energy + reserveEnergy(s.cfg, a);
    17	
    18	export interface FreeRow {
    19	  id: string;
    20	  /** Lattice free energy E, and total energy including the reserve, at the stated phase. */
    21	  freeE: string;
    22	  total: string;
    23	  /** total(b) - total(arm): gap > 0 means the comparator holds less total energy than b (weaker on total), gap < 0 more. */
    24	  constructionGap: string;
    25	  /** Free-energy difference to b (arm - b). */
    26	  freeGapToB: string;
    27	  flag: "comparator-excess" | "comparator-deficit" | "total-matched" | "reference";
    28	}
    29	
    30	export function freeRows(arms: readonly PreArm[]): FreeRow[] {
    31	  const b = arms.find((x) => x.id === "b");
    32	  if (!b) return [];
    33	  const bTotal = totalEnergyOf(b.state, b.audit), bFree = latticeE(b.state);
    34	  return arms.map((x) => {
    35	    const total = totalEnergyOf(x.state, x.audit), free = latticeE(x.state);
    36	    const gap = bTotal - total;
    37	    return { id: x.id, freeE: `${free}`, total: `${total}`, constructionGap: `${gap}`, freeGapToB: `${free - bFree}`, flag: x.id === "b" ? "reference" : gap < 0n ? "comparator-excess" : gap > 0n ? "comparator-deficit" : "total-matched" };
    38	  });
    39	}
    40	
    41	/** A receiver-alone lattice with b's matter spread uniformly as nutrient A and a caller-chosen E field; reserve as b's. Own audit baseline. */
    42	function freshLattice(b: PreArm, id: FreeId, efield: (n: number) => number[]): PreArm {
    43	  const cfg = b.state.cfg;
    44	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    45	  const s = allocState(cfg);
    46	  const matter = totalsOf(cfg, b.state.cells).matter;
    47	  const base = matter / BigInt(n), rem = Number(matter % BigInt(n));
    48	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[CH.A * n + i] = Number(base) + (i < rem ? 1 : 0);
    49	  const e = efield(n);
    50	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[CH.E * n + i] = e[i];
    51	  s.cells.fill(MOT_ZERO, CH.MOT * n, (CH.MOT + 1) * n);
    52	  s.step = b.state.step;
    53	  const audit: Audit = { energyStart: 0n, matterStart: 0n, reserveB: b.audit.reserveB, reserveE: b.audit.reserveE, heatAnalytic: 0n };
    54	  return { id, state: s, audit, records: [] };
    55	}
    56	
    57	/** Own baseline: the constructed start (lattice plus reserve, no analytic heat, lightIn and heatOut zero). */
    58	function openOwnBaseline(x: PreArm): void {
    59	  const t = totalsOf(x.state.cfg, x.state.cells);
    60	  x.audit.energyStart = t.energy + reserveEnergy(x.state.cfg, x.audit);
    61	  x.audit.matterStart = t.matter + BigInt(x.audit.reserveB);
    62	  assertAudit(x.state, x.audit, `${x.id} start`);
    63	  const errs = validateState(x.state);
    64	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`credit-overdraw: ${x.id} fails validateState: ${errs[0]}`);
    65	}
    66	
    67	export const uniformSplit = (total: bigint, n: number): number[] => {
    68	  const base = total / BigInt(n), rem = Number(total % BigInt(n));
    69	  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => Number(base) + (i < rem ? 1 : 0));
    70	};
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * fB: fS plus the biomass the field held in the footprint-plus-halo region (the first rung's d1B quantity), handed over in the reserve
    74	 * as bound matter taken from the lattice outside the region. NO lattice energy debit, so free E stays equal to b cell for cell
    75	 * before and after placement; the extra bound energy (eB - eA) * dB is extra total energy (gap < 0, comparator-excess).
    76	 */
    77	export function fundBiomass(f: PreArm, dB: number, outside: readonly number[]): void {
    78	  try {
    79	    debitEven(f.state, CH.A, dB, outside);
    80	  } catch (e) {
    81	    if (e instanceof NicheInvalid) throw new NicheInvalid(`credit-overdraw: matched biomass ${dB} cannot be funded from the lattice outside the region (${e.reason})`);
    82	    throw e;
    83	  }
    84	  f.audit.reserveB += dB;
    85	}
    86	
    87	export interface FreeMatched {
    88	  arms: PreArm[];
    89	  /** Comparators that could not be built (refused, never clamped): id and reason. */
    90	  refused: { id: FreeId; reason: string }[];
    91	  dB: number;
    92	}
    93	
    94	/** Build fU, fS and fB around the conditioned builder-removed arm `b` (before placement). A refused one is reported, not dropped silently. */
    95	export function buildFreeMatched(b: PreArm, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): FreeMatched {
    96	  const arms: PreArm[] = [];
    97	  const refused: FreeMatched["refused"] = [];
    98	  const n = cellCount(b.state.cfg);
    99	  const bE = latticeE(b.state);
   100	  const copyE = (): number[] => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => b.state.cells[CH.E * n + i]);
   101	  const region = regionCells(p), outside = outsideRegion(p);
   102	  const sumB = (cells: readonly number[]) => cells.reduce((t, i) => t + b.state.cells[CH.B * n + i], 0);
   103	  const dB = sumB(region);
   104	  const attempt = (id: FreeId, make: () => PreArm): void => {
   105	    try {
   106	      const f = make();
   107	      openOwnBaseline(f);
   108	      arms.push(f);
   109	    } catch (e) {
   110	      if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   111	      refused.push({ id, reason: e.reason });
   112	    }
   113	  };
   114	  attempt("fU", () => freshLattice(b, "fU", () => uniformSplit(bE, n)));
   115	  attempt("fS", () => freshLattice(b, "fS", copyE));
   116	  attempt("fB", () => {
   117	    const f = freshLattice(b, "fB", copyE);
   118	    fundBiomass(f, dB, outside);
   119	    return f;
   120	  });
   121	  return { arms, refused, dB };
   122	}

    61	
    62	/** Wall pass at a dose for a candidate: every gating contrast positive in at least wallPassMin seeds and median >= delta. */
    63	export function wallPasses(row: DoseRow, delta: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
    64	  return GATING_FE.every((k) => row.stats[k].k >= p.wallPassMin && row.stats[k].medianTimes2 >= 2 * delta);
    65	}
    66	/** G-NOISE: delta at most 1/noiseDivisor of the limiting contrast's median effect (twice-median integers: medianTimes2 >= 2 * noiseDivisor * delta). */
    67	export function noiseGate(row: DoseRow, delta: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
    68	  return row.limitingMedianTimes2 >= 2 * p.noiseDivisor * delta;
    69	}
    70	
    71	export interface Qualification {
    72	  cluster: number;
    73	  delta: number;
    74	  /** 2 x the median dev builder polymer at T1 = twice-the-median integer builderPMedianTimes2. */
    75	  ceiling: number;
    76	  /** Smallest dose at which the wall passes ignoring the ceiling (the minimum detectable dose), or null if none on the ladder. */
    77	  minDetectableDose: number | null;
    78	  /** Smallest dose <= ceiling where the wall passes and G-NOISE holds, or null. */
    79	  qualifyingDose: number | null;
    80	  /** Doses <= ceiling on the ladder. */
    81	  dosesWithinCeiling: number[];
    82	  qualified: boolean;
    83	  reason: string;
    84	}
    85	
    86	export function qualify(cluster: number, delta: number, builderPMedianTimes2: number, rows: readonly DoseRow[], p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): Qualification {
    87	  const ceiling = builderPMedianTimes2;
    88	  const within = rows.filter((r) => r.totalP <= ceiling);
    89	  const mdd = rows.find((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p)) ?? null;
    90	  const q = within.find((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p) && noiseGate(r, delta, p)) ?? null;
    91	  const reason = q
    92	    ? `W1 passes at ${q.totalP} (<= ceiling ${ceiling}) and delta ${delta} is at most half of the limiting contrast (${q.limiting}) median effect`
    93	    : mdd === null
    94	      ? `W1 is not detected at any ladder dose up to ${rows.length ? rows[rows.length - 1].totalP : 0} (passing needs ${p.wallPassMin} of ${p.wallSeeds.length} seeds positive on all four contrasts and median >= delta ${delta})`
    95	      : mdd.totalP > ceiling
    96	        ? `minimum detectable dose ${mdd.totalP} exceeds the realistic ceiling ${ceiling} (2 x dev builder polymer median)`
    97	        : `W1 passes at ${mdd.totalP} but delta ${delta} exceeds half of the limiting contrast (${mdd.limiting}) median effect ${mdd.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2} (G-NOISE)`;
    98	  return { cluster, delta, ceiling, minDetectableDose: mdd?.totalP ?? null, qualifyingDose: q?.totalP ?? null, dosesWithinCeiling: within.map((r) => r.totalP), qualified: q !== null, reason };
    99	}

   101	export interface LadderReport {
   102	  geometry: "W1";
   103	  rows: DoseRow[];
   104	  w2: { status: "unavailable"; reason: string } | { status: "available"; reason: string };
   105	}
     1	// I06b edge decision (PLAN section 5.3): the first rung's exact integer sign tests, with 16 blocks, inoc2 in place of inoc, a viability
     2	// requirement on the field arm (no survival-benefit claim), and adequacy per candidate from the ordinary-placement positive control.
     3	// The first rung's decision.ts is not touched.
     4	import type { EvidenceStatus } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { ALPHA, contrastResult, ratLe, ratMax, signTestP, type ContrastResult, type Rat } from "./decision.ts";
     6	import { GATING_FE, type GatingFe } from "./protocol.ts";
     7	
     8	export interface CandidateInputFe {
     9	  id: string;
    10	  /** "not-simulated": the positive control did not qualify this candidate, so no fresh block was run. */
    11	  status: "ok" | "invalid" | "not-simulated";
    12	  invalidReasons: string[];
    13	  contrasts: Record<GatingFe, number[]> & { spaceP: number[]; spaceNP: number[]; inoc: number[]; naive: number[] };
    14	  delta: number;
    15	  /** Blocks in which the field arm was viable (receiver present in at least R - 1 of R replicates). */
    16	  viableBlocks: number;
    17	  blocks: number;
    18	  expectedBlocks: number;
    19	  /** Reasons when not simulated: minimum detectable dose, ceiling, floor. */
    20	  notSimulatedReasons: string[];
    21	}
    22	
    23	export interface CandidateResultFe {
    24	  id: string;
    25	  label: EvidenceStatus;
    26	  simulated: boolean;
    27	  mechanism: "polymer-arrangement-attributed" | "non-polymer field pattern" | "combined conditioned field" | "n/a";
    28	  reasons: string[];
    29	  gating: Record<GatingFe, ContrastResult>;
    30	  attribution: { spaceP: ContrastResult; spaceNP: ContrastResult };
    31	  /** Reported only: the first rung's inoculum contrast and the naive contrast. */
    32	  reported: { inoc: ContrastResult; naive: ContrastResult };
    33	  pCand: Rat;
    34	  holmThreshold: Rat | null;
    35	  holmRejected: boolean;
    36	  viabilityPass: boolean;
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function decideCandidatesFe(cands: readonly CandidateInputFe[], minViable = 14): CandidateResultFe[] {
    40	  const ran = cands.filter((c) => c.status !== "not-simulated");
    41	  const m = ran.length;
    42	  const alphaEach: Rat = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * Math.max(1, m) };
    43	  const results = new Map<string, CandidateResultFe>();
    44	  const raw = (d: readonly number[], delta: number): ContrastResult => contrastResult(d, delta, { num: 0, den: 1 });
    45	  for (const c of cands) {
    46	    const g = Object.fromEntries(GATING_FE.map((k) => [k, raw(c.contrasts[k], c.delta)])) as Record<GatingFe, ContrastResult>;
    47	    results.set(c.id, {
    48	      id: c.id,
    49	      label: "unsupported-measurement",
    50	      simulated: c.status !== "not-simulated",
    51	      mechanism: "n/a",
    52	      reasons: [],
    53	      gating: g,
    54	      attribution: { spaceP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceP, c.delta, alphaEach), spaceNP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceNP, c.delta, alphaEach) },
    55	      reported: { inoc: raw(c.contrasts.inoc, c.delta), naive: raw(c.contrasts.naive, c.delta) },
    56	      pCand: c.status === "ok" && c.blocks === c.expectedBlocks ? ratMax(GATING_FE.map((k) => g[k].p)) : { num: 1, den: 1 },
    57	      holmThreshold: null,
    58	      holmRejected: false,
    59	      viabilityPass: c.viableBlocks >= minViable,
    60	    });
    61	  }
    62	  const order = ran.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!).sort((a, b) => a.pCand.num * b.pCand.den - b.pCand.num * a.pCand.den || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1));
    63	  let stopped = false;
    64	  order.forEach((r, i) => {
    65	    r.holmThreshold = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * (m - i) };
    66	    if (!stopped && ratLe(r.pCand, r.holmThreshold)) r.holmRejected = true;
    67	    else stopped = true;
    68	  });
    69	  for (const c of ran) {
    70	    const r = results.get(c.id)!;
    71	    for (const k of GATING_FE) r.gating[k].passes = ratLe(r.gating[k].p, r.holmThreshold!) && r.gating[k].medianTimes2 >= 2 * c.delta;
    72	  }
    73	  for (const c of cands) {
    74	    const r = results.get(c.id)!;
    75	    if (c.status === "not-simulated") {
    76	      r.label = "unsupported-measurement";
    77	      r.reasons = c.notSimulatedReasons.length ? c.notSimulatedReasons : ["the ordinary-placement positive control did not qualify this candidate; no fresh block was simulated"];
    78	    } else if (c.status === "invalid" || c.blocks !== c.expectedBlocks) {
    79	      r.label = "invalid-or-incomplete";
    80	      r.reasons = c.invalidReasons.length ? c.invalidReasons : [`${c.blocks} of ${c.expectedBlocks} blocks valid`];
    81	    } else {
    82	      const failed = GATING_FE.filter((k) => !r.gating[k].passes);
    83	      const ok = r.holmRejected && failed.length === 0 && r.viabilityPass;
    84	      r.label = ok ? "supported" : "unsupported-within-tested-domain";
    85	      if (!ok) {
    86	        if (failed.length) r.reasons.push(`contrast(s) not passing: ${failed.join(", ")}`);
    87	        if (!r.holmRejected) r.reasons.push("Holm does not reject the candidate");
    88	        if (!r.viabilityPass) r.reasons.push(`field arm viable in fewer than ${minViable} blocks`);
    89	      } else {
    90	        r.mechanism = r.attribution.spaceP.passes && !r.attribution.spaceNP.passes ? "polymer-arrangement-attributed" : !r.attribution.spaceP.passes && r.attribution.spaceNP.passes ? "non-polymer field pattern" : "combined conditioned field";
    91	      }
    92	    }
    93	  }
    94	  return cands.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!);
    95	}
    96	
    97	export type PanelVerdictFe = "causal-benefit-detected" | "no-causal-benefit-detected" | "invalid-or-incomplete" | "unsupported-measurement";
    98	export interface PanelResultFe {
    99	  verdict: PanelVerdictFe;
   100	  text: string;
   101	  supported: string[];
   102	  negative: string[];
   103	  unusable: string[];
   104	}
   105	
   106	export function panelVerdictFe(results: readonly CandidateResultFe[]): PanelResultFe {
   107	  const supported = results.filter((r) => r.label === "supported").map((r) => r.id);
   108	  const negative = results.filter((r) => r.label === "unsupported-within-tested-domain").map((r) => r.id);
   109	  const unusable = results.filter((r) => r.label === "invalid-or-incomplete" || r.label === "unsupported-measurement").map((r) => r.id);
   110	  if (supported.length) return { verdict: "causal-benefit-detected", text: `Dependency edge supported for ${supported.join(", ")} within this domain.${unusable.length ? ` Not usable: ${unusable.join(", ")}.` : ""}`, supported, negative, unusable };
   111	  if (negative.length > 0 && unusable.length === 0) return { verdict: "no-causal-benefit-detected", text: `No causal benefit of size at least delta detected for ${negative.join(", ")} within the tested panel, horizon and field; the positive control qualified each.`, supported, negative, unusable };
   112	  const anyInvalid = results.some((r) => r.label === "invalid-or-incomplete");
   113	  return { verdict: anyInvalid ? "invalid-or-incomplete" : "unsupported-measurement", text: `No verdict: usable negatives ${negative.length ? negative.join(", ") : "none"}; not usable ${unusable.join(", ") || "none"}.`, supported, negative, unusable };
   114	}
   115	
   116	/** Exact P(Binomial(16, q) >= k) for q = qn/qd as a reduced-by-nothing rational of BigInts (tests compare it with the frozen power statement). */
   117	export function binomialUpperTail(n: number, k: number, qn: bigint, qd: bigint): { num: bigint; den: bigint } {
   118	  const choose = (a: number, b: number): bigint => {
   119	    let c = 1n;
   120	    for (let i = 1; i <= b; i++) c = (c * BigInt(a - b + i)) / BigInt(i);
   121	    return c;
   122	  };
   123	  let num = 0n;
   124	  for (let j = k; j <= n; j++) num += choose(n, j) * qn ** BigInt(j) * (qd - qn) ** BigInt(n - j);
   125	  return { num, den: qd ** BigInt(n) };
   126	}

    36	  if (hooked || process.env.VITEST) return;
    37	  hooked = true;
    38	  try {
    39	    registerHooks({
    40	      resolve(specifier, context, next) {

    42	        return m ? next(pathToFileURL(`${REPO}packages/${m[1]}/src/index.ts`).href, context) : next(specifier, context);
    43	      },
    44	    });
    45	  } catch { /* already running under a loader that resolves @bl/* (vitest) */ }
    46	}

    61	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
    62	export const sha256 = async (text: string | Uint8Array): Promise<string> => {
    63	  const { schema } = await lib();
    64	  return schema.sha256HexJS(typeof text === "string" ? new TextEncoder().encode(text) : text);

    66	/**
    67	 * Reservations made by slices after this one are not part of this protocol's registry binding: the digest is of the registry as it stood
    68	 * when this slice froze, so a later slice reserving its own seeds does not invalidate this evidence. Collisions with those reservations
    69	 * are still checked against the live registry below. A further slice adds its owner prefix here (or scopes its own digest to its own reservations, as I08 does).
    70	 */
    71	const LATER_SLICE_OWNERS = ["i08 moving light", "i06b free-energy-matched"];
    72	export const registryDigest = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => {
    73	  const text = readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8");
    74	  return sha256(text.split("\n").filter((line) => !LATER_SLICE_OWNERS.some((o) => line.includes(`"owner": "${o}`))).join("\n"));
    75	};

    77	/** Files whose bytes feed the evidence: the library, the simulator and schema it reads, and this tool. */
    78	const SOURCE_DIRS = [SRC_DIR, "packages/schema/src/", "packages/sim-ref/src/"];
    79	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
    80	  const dirs = SOURCE_DIRS.flatMap((d) => readdirSync(`${root}${d}`).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f));
    81	  return [...dirs, "tools/niche/niche.ts"];
    82	}
    83	
    84	export async function generate(): Promise<unknown> {
    85	  const L = await lib();
    86	  return L.buildPayload(await registryDigest());
    87	}
    88	
    89	export async function makeManifest(payload: any, evidenceText: string, seconds: number): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    90	  const sources: Record<string, string> = {};
    91	  for (const f of sourceFiles()) sources[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(REPO + f));
    92	  return {
    93	    kind: "niche-evidence-manifest",
    94	    payloadFileSha256: await sha256(evidenceText),
    95	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
    96	    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
    97	    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
    98	    stepProjection: payload.stepProjection,
    99	    sources,
   100	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", baseCommit: BASE_COMMIT, node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
   101	  };
   102	}
   103	
   104	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
   105	export async function checkSaved(paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE, manifest: MANIFEST, calibration: CALIBRATION, lock: LOCK }, root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
   106	  const L = await lib();
   107	  const problems: string[] = [];
   108	  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
   109	  if (problems.length) return problems;
   110	  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
   111	  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
   112	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
   113	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
   114	  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
   115	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
   116	  if (!L.verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
   117	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   118	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   119	  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
   120	  // The protocol was frozen (lock written) before the fresh run, and nothing changed since.
   121	  const digest = await registryDigest(root);
   122	  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("seed-registry.json changed since the evidence was written");
   123	  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
   124	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHash(digest, L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
   125	  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
   126	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon({ dev: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, replicates: L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, wall: L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, fresh: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds })) problems.push("lock seed lists differ from the protocol");
   127	  // Dev results are reported separately and match the calibration file the protocol was frozen on.
   128	  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.dev)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the dev section of evidence.json");
   129	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
   130	  for (const c of payload.dev.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) problems.push(`delta for cluster ${c.cluster} (${L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)]}) is not the dev replicate floor (${c.deltaProposed})`);
   131	  // Seed hygiene.
   132	  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
   133	  const all = [...L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds, ...L.FIXTURE_SEEDS].map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
   134	  const own = "i06 niche construction";
   135	  const foreign = L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(own)), own);
   136	  for (const f of foreign) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
   137	  // Decision recomputation from the stored inputs.
   138	  const recomputed = payload.fresh.ran === false ? payload.fresh.results : L.decideCandidates(payload.fresh.candidates.map((c: any) => c.input), payload.dev.wall.positiveControlPassed);
   139	  if (canon(recomputed) !== canon(payload.fresh.results)) problems.push("fresh results do not follow the stated decision rules");
   140	  if (canon(L.panelVerdict(recomputed)) !== canon(payload.fresh.panel)) problems.push("panel verdict does not follow the stated rules");
   141	  // Every ledger exactly zero.
   142	  const blocks = [...payload.dev.candidates, ...payload.fresh.candidates].flatMap((c: any) => c.blocks);
   143	  for (const b of blocks) for (const [arm, recs] of Object.entries<any[]>(b.transfers)) for (const r of recs) if (r.residual !== "0" || r.matterResidual !== "0") problems.push(`block ${b.seed} arm ${arm} op ${r.op}: nonzero residual`);
   144	  // Manifest sources.
   145	  const listed = Object.keys(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>);
   146	  if (existsSync(root + SRC_DIR)) for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) if (!listed.includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
   147	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries<string>(manifest.sources)) {
   148	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
   149	    else if ((await sha256(readFileSync(root + file))) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
   150	  }
   151	  return problems;
   152	}
   153	
   154	export async function replayCheck(evidencePath = EVIDENCE): Promise<string[]> {
   155	  const fresh = serialize(await generate());
   156	  return fresh === readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   157	}
   158	
   159	async function bench(): Promise<void> {
   160	  const L = await lib();
   161	  const t0 = Date.now();
   162	  const r = L.runBlock(L.PROTOCOL.panel[0], L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds[0], L.PROTOCOL, L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds[0]);
   163	  const ms = Date.now() - t0;
   164	  const steps = 2 * L.PROTOCOL.T1 + 12 * L.PROTOCOL.T2; // 2 histories, 10 arms and 2 replicate arms (all steps incl. fixtures are in the projection)
   165	  const proj = L.stepProjection();
   166	  console.log(`block valid=${r.valid}: ${steps} steps in ${ms} ms = ${(ms / steps).toFixed(2)} ms/step`);
   167	  console.log(`projected regeneration: ${proj.steps} steps (${proj.formula}) ~ ${Math.round((proj.steps * ms) / steps / 1000)} s`);
   168	}
   169	
   170	async function main(): Promise<void> {
    34	## 4. Design
    35	
    36	Everything in rung-1 PLAN section 4 holds unless stated: single 24x24 tile, kernelRadius 5, mutation off, ladder genes, recurring injury with shell, T1 = 60, T2 = 80, reserve R = (480, 960), footprint (14,12) r3, halo 2, absolute clock T1 for every Stage-2 arm, candidates by panel `{0, 4, 9}` and receiver cluster 12, drop order 9 then 4 by runtime only.
    37	
    38	### 4.1 Free-energy-matched comparators (answers defect 1)
    39	
    40	Free energy means lattice channel E (`sum E`), reported next to total energy (`totalsOf`), for the builder-removed arm `b` and each comparator, at the start of Stage 2 (before inoculum placement) and after placement. Matter is identical in every arm (lattice plus reserve), as in rung 1. Observed rung-1 direction (seed 8600501, cluster 0): the receiver-alone control d0 held MORE free E than `b` (22,169 vs 9,917) with equal total (26,969), because `b` holds its energy as bound matter.
    41	
    42	Matching free energy and total energy simultaneously is impossible at equal matter when the comparator has no conditioned composition (bound matter carries energy at eB, eP, eC, eA). Each comparator is a separately constructed initial state (not a transformation of `b` inside a run); its own ledger starts from its own total and conserves exactly. The difference is reported as `constructionGap = totalEnergy(b) - totalEnergy(f)` (signed BigInt, evidence only, never in the ledger): **gap > 0** means the comparator holds less total energy than the field arm; **gap < 0** means it holds more (a stronger comparator, the conservative side for the claim).
    43	
    44	- **fU (uniform free-matched):** receiver-alone lattice, uniform A, free E set uniform to `sum E(b)` over the lattice.
    45	- **fS (spatial free-matched):** as fU but E copied cell for cell from `b` (same free-energy field, no polymer, no biomass, matter all A in the remainder). Isolates "the energy pattern alone" from "structure".
    46	- **fB (free-matched plus biomass-matched):** fS plus the rung-1 d1B raise of B (region plus halo, matter converted from A), **without** the `(eB - eA)*dB` lattice debit, so free E stays equal to `b` cell for cell before and after placement; the bound-energy increase is entered as extra total energy (gap < 0, flagged `comparator-excess`). Test: free-E equality before and after placement.
    47	- Rung-1 d0, d1E, d1B are kept unchanged and reported (total-matched family).
    48	
    49	New inoculum contrast: `D_inoc2 = Y(b | builder) - max(Y(d1E), Y(d1B), Y(fU), Y(fS), Y(fB))`; every constructed comparator is in the max (none excluded by sign of gap; `comparator-excess` and `comparator-deficit` are labels in evidence). Rung-1 `D_inoc` is still computed and reported next to it. The four gating contrasts are builder, poly, space, inoc2. A comparator whose construction would need negative lattice E or exceed `POOL_MAX`/`MATTER_MAX` is refused (`credit-overdraw`, never clamped); a block where fU, fS and fB are all refused is `invalid-or-incomplete` for that candidate (reported, never dropped).
    50	
    51	### 4.2 Better-powered readout (answers defect 2)
    52	
    53	- **Replicated Stage 2.** Each arm's Stage 2 is run for `R = 4` sim seeds (block seed plus fixed offsets `{0, 1, 2, 3} * 1000`), identical offsets in every arm (so injury steps and RNG keys still coincide across arms); Stage 1 histories and transfers are shared. Primary endpoint `Y = sum over the R replicates of receiver-labelled B at T2` (integer; no division, units are x R of rung 1). Viability: receiver B above zero in at least `R - 1` of R replicates counts the field arm `b` as viable for that block. Viability is a requirement for the gate (field arm viable in at least 14 of 16 blocks; at a true per-block rate of 0.9, 15/16 would pass only 51% of the time). A paired survival contrast (arm vs comparator) is reported as attribution, never gating, and no survival benefit is claimed.
    54	- **Replicate seeds** are enumerated and reserved explicitly (5.1) for the maximum R of 8.
    55	- **More blocks:** 16 fresh blocks per candidate (was 8). Exact sign test over 16; `k = 16` gives 1/65,536, `k = 14` gives 137/65,536, `k = 12` gives 2,517/65,536 (hand-enumerated in tests). Candidate threshold by Holm at alpha 0.05 over 3 candidates as before (1/60, 1/40, 1/20 for the three ordered p-values).
    56	- **Power statement (frozen wording).** The first Holm rejection needs k >= 13 of 16 (p = 697/65,536). A contrast whose true per-block positive probability is 0.75 passes it only 40.5% of the time, 0.85 gives 79%, 0.90 gives 93%, 0.95 gives 99%. The design therefore detects consistent benefits (about 0.9 or higher of blocks positive and median above delta); a negative means "no benefit of size at least delta in at least about 90% of blocks", not "no benefit". Four contrasts, delta and viability can only lower the pass rate. Dev reports the empirical positive-block rate of the wall control at the qualifying dose next to this table.
    57	- **Delta from a replicate control with the same readout:** `delta = max(ceil(0.02 * B_r * R), 1.5 * max|D|)` over dev replicate pairs (nonbuilder vs nonbuilder, builder vs builder, on a disjoint replicate seed set of R sims). With the sum over R the expected floor falls by about sqrt(R) relative to rung 1 after rescaling; dev says whether it did.
    58	- Secondary readout (reported, never gating): per-replicate area under the receiver-B census curve (sum over the 8 censuses), as a lower-variance cross-check.
    59	- **Noise gate (G-NOISE, dev only, before any fresh seed), per candidate:** `delta_c` must be at most half of the wall control's median effect on the **limiting gating contrast** (the one with the smallest median effect among the four), in summed-R units, at that candidate's qualifying dose (4.3). Otherwise the rung stops `unsupported-measurement` and reports the achieved floor and the minimum detectable dose; the fresh seeds are not read.
    60	
    61	### 4.3 Positive control at realistic scale (answers defect 3)
    62	
    63	The hand-wall fixtures (W1 ring, W2 floor under the receiver; rung-1 definitions) run the full four-contrast gate on dev wall seeds across a dose ladder of total polymer `P in {60, 120, 240, 600, 1200}`, ascending, stopping each wall at its first passing dose (that dose is its minimum detectable dose); doses above the ceiling are run only if nothing at or below it passes, to state the minimum detectable dose.
    64	
    65	- **Per-candidate realistic ceiling** `C_c = 2 x` the median dev builder polymer at T1 of candidate c (rung 1 measured 84.5-103.5, so C is about 170-207, which admits 60 and 120, not 240). The control for candidate c uses doses `<= C_c` only.
    66	- **Ordinary placement required.** The rung-1 `wallGateBlock` uses `allowP: true` (overwriting polymer on injured cells), which real transfers (`placeAll` default false) forbid. The qualifying control must place the wall by the ordinary placement rule (no overwrite of existing polymer, same pool-cap and reserve guards) at cells where that is legal; wall variants that need `allowP: true` stay as a reported diagnostic and never qualify the assay. If no ordinary-placement wall geometry exists, the control is `unavailable` and the stated reason is that none exists, ending the rung `unsupported-measurement`.
    67	- **Wall pass rule (dev, 8 wall seeds):** for each of the four gating contrasts, at least 7 of 8 seeds positive (exact p = 9/256) and median >= `delta_c`. A wall passes at dose P iff all four hold. The control **qualifies candidate c** iff W1 or W2 passes at some dose `<= C_c` and G-NOISE holds (4.2).
    68	- A candidate that does not qualify ends `unsupported-measurement` with the minimum detectable dose, its ratio to the builder polymer and the floor that explains it; its fresh blocks are not simulated (a sealed stopped payload is still written, section 8). Candidates qualify independently.
    69	
    70	Dropped from this rung (cut for scope, section 11): the builder founder-biomass dose-response knob.
    71	
    72	### 4.4 Readouts, ledger and flux (unchanged from rung 1, per replicate)
    73	
    74	Per arm and replicate: receiver survival, receiver-labelled biomass inventory, flux attribution (synthesis share; "growth" only at share at least half and no saturated counter), builder cost, before/after inventories. Ledger invariant per arm: lattice + reserve + cumulative heatAnalytic against that arm's own starting total, exact `0n` at every census of both stages, including every replicate (the unchanged rung-1 audit; no credit term). Evidence adds per arm: free E, total energy, `constructionGap` vs `b`.
    75	
    76	### 4.5 Fixtures
    77	
    78	16x16, kernelRadius 5 at unit scale (rung-1 constraint); the positive control is additionally qualified at the frozen scale on dev.
    79	- `far-field-energy` (new, 2.1: fools rung-1 `D_inoc`, not `D_inoc2`; full gate rejects via `D_poly = 0`); also a uniform and an in-footprint variant, `caughtBy` recorded.
    80	- `free-gap-replay`: rung-1 observed direction at fixture scale (d0 holds MORE free E than `b` with equal totals; bound inventories as recorded); asserts fU/fS/fB reproduce `b`'s free E exactly (difference zero), that d0's excess is removed, and that `constructionGap` is recorded with every arm's own ledger residual `0n`.
    81	- `takeover-only`, `null-field`, `hand-wall-gate-W2` (rung-1 fixtures re-run with the second protocol; expectations unchanged).
    82	- Guards re-run: overwrite, pool cap, underfunded reserve, exhausted reserve, plus `credit-overdraw` (a free-matched comparator that would need negative lattice E or exceed a cap is refused, not clamped).
    83	
    84	## 5. Pre-stated rules (frozen before any fresh-seed run; dated amendment in section 10)
    85	
    86	### 5.1 Seeds
    87	Block seeds in `8,601,001-8,601,999` and every derived replicate seed are reserved in `experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json` (owner `i06b free-energy-matched`) **before** anything runs. Replicate sims use block seed + `k * 1000`, `k = 0..7` (R = 4 uses k = 0..3; R = 8 uses k = 0..7), so derived seeds reach 8,608,999: the reservation lists the contiguous range `8,601,001-8,608,999`; the implementer first runs `seedCollisions` over it and, if any existing owner collides, replaces the offset by a collision-free stride and records that in the amendment. The protocol hash binds the explicit enumerated list of every seed that can run (block, replicate, noise, wall, fixture) and the registry digest; `--check` rescans the enumerated list, not only base lists. Never reused: 8,600,101-108, 8,600,501-508, nor any other rung-1 seed. Dev blocks 8,601,001-8,601,008, dev replicate-noise 8,601,201-8,601,204, wall 8,601,301-8,601,308, fixtures 8,601,401-8,601,408, fresh 8,601,501-8,601,516. Dev outcomes may set only `T1, T2` (unchanged unless dev shows cause), `delta_c`, the dose rungs and ceilings, and `R` (4 or 8; one escalation to 8 allowed if the noise gate fails, recorded before the freeze). Fresh seeds are read once, after the freeze commit is pushed and verified (section 9).
    88	
    89	### 5.2 Estimands
    90	Primary `Y` as in 4.2, paired within a block. Gating contrasts: `D_builder`, `D_poly`, `D_space`, `D_inoc2`. Attribution only: `D_spaceP`, `D_spaceNP`, rung-1 `D_inoc`, `D_naive = Y(b) - Y(d0)`.
    91	
    92	### 5.3 Edge label
    93	Rung-1 rules with these changes only: 16 blocks; `inoc2` replaces `inoc`; per-contrast exact one-sided sign test (zeros non-positive) with `median >= delta`; field arm viable in at least 14 of 16 blocks (viability requirement, no survival-benefit claim); candidate p = max over four; Holm across candidates that ran. Adequacy is per candidate: the ordinary-placement positive control qualified that candidate at a dose within its ceiling and G-NOISE held (4.3); the rung-1 polymer criterion is a reported field only. `unsupported-within-tested-domain` only when valid, adequately measured and a gating test fails; its meaning is bounded by the power statement in 4.2. Mechanism text and the three-valued panel verdict as rung 1.
    94

    84	  });

    88	    expect(verifySealFe(p)).toBe(false);

    15	/** A conditioned arm with bound energy (detritus) and the comparators built around it, at unit scale. */
    16	const world = (seed = 1) => {
    17	  const { base, cond } = detritusField(seed, fp);
    18	  const arms = [...controlsAround(cond, base, seed, fp)];
    19	  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], fp);
    20	  return { base, cond, arms: [...arms, ...fm.arms], fm };

    22	const byId = (arms: PreArm[], id: string) => arms.find((x) => x.id === id)!;
    23	const chan = (s: PreArm, ch: number) => Array.from(s.state.cells.slice(ch * cellCount(s.state.cfg), (ch + 1) * cellCount(s.state.cfg)));
    24	
    25	describe("free-energy-matched comparators", () => {
    26	  it("fU free E equals b free E exactly and matter equals", () => {
    27	    const { arms } = world();
    28	    const b = byId(arms, "b"), f = byId(arms, "fU");
    29	    expect(latticeE(f.state)).toBe(latticeE(b.state));
    30	    expect(latticeE(byId(arms, "d0").state)).toBeGreaterThan(latticeE(b.state));
    31	    const mb = residuals(b.state, b.audit), mf = residuals(f.state, f.audit);
    32	    expect(mb.matter).toBe(0n);
    33	    expect(mf.matter).toBe(0n);
    34	    expect(f.audit.matterStart).toBe(b.audit.matterStart);
    35	    expect(f.audit.reserveB).toBe(b.audit.reserveB);
    36	    expect(f.audit.reserveE).toBe(b.audit.reserveE);
    37	  });
    38	
    39	  it("fS E channel equals b E channel cell for cell", () => {
    40	    const { arms } = world();
    41	    expect(chan(byId(arms, "fS"), CH.E)).toEqual(chan(byId(arms, "b"), CH.E));
    42	  });
    43	
    44	  it("fB free E equals b cell for cell before and after placement (no lattice debit, gap < 0 flagged comparator-excess)", () => {
    45	    const { arms } = world();
    46	    const fB = byId(arms, "fB"), b = byId(arms, "b");
    47	    expect(chan(fB, CH.E)).toEqual(chan(b, CH.E));
    48	    expect(fB.audit.reserveB).toBeGreaterThan(b.audit.reserveB);
    49	    const rows = freeRows(arms);
    50	    expect(rows.find((r) => r.id === "fB")!.freeGapToB).toBe("0");
    51	    placeAll(arms, fp, { genome: undefined });
    52	    expect(chan(fB, CH.E)).toEqual(chan(b, CH.E));
    53	    expect(freeRows(arms).find((r) => r.id === "fB")!.freeGapToB).toBe("0");
    54	    // On the cells where both hold bound B the difference is the extra inoculum, as total energy.
    55	    expect(BigInt(freeRows(arms).find((r) => r.id === "fB")!.constructionGap)).toBeLessThan(BigInt(freeRows(arms).find((r) => r.id === "fS")!.constructionGap));
    56	  });
    57	
    58	  it("fB carries comparator-excess against total energy when the reserve it adds outweighs the field's bound deficit", () => {
    59	    // A free-field world (gift): b = d0 + free energy, no bound matter; fB adds the biomass b held (none) so equals fS: total-matched.
    60	    const { base } = world();
    61	    const cond: PreArm = { id: "b", state: base.state, audit: base.audit, records: [] };
    62	    const fm = buildFreeMatched(cond, fp);
    63	    const rows = freeRows([cond, ...fm.arms]);
    64	    for (const id of ["fU", "fS", "fB"]) expect(rows.find((r) => r.id === id)!.flag).toBe("total-matched");
    65	  });
    66	
    67	  it("constructionGap sign: positive when the comparator total is lower than the field arm's", () => {
    68	    const { arms } = world();
    69	    const rows = freeRows(arms);
    70	    const fU = rows.find((r) => r.id === "fU")!;
    71	    // detritus B holds eB per quantum, nutrient A only eA: matter equal, so the comparator holds less total energy.
    72	    expect(BigInt(fU.constructionGap)).toBeGreaterThan(0n);
    73	    expect(fU.flag).toBe("comparator-deficit");
    74	    expect(BigInt(fU.constructionGap)).toBe(totalEnergyOf(byId(arms, "b").state, byId(arms, "b").audit) - totalEnergyOf(byId(arms, "fU").state, byId(arms, "fU").audit));
    75	  });
    76	
    77	  it("every arm residual is 0n against its own baseline, at construction and after placement", () => {
    78	    const { arms } = world();
    79	    const check = () => {
    80	      for (const x of arms) {
    81	        const r = residuals(x.state, x.audit);
    82	        expect([x.id, r.energy, r.matter]).toEqual([x.id, 0n, 0n]);
    83	      }
    84	    };
    85	    check();
    86	    placeAll(arms, fp);
    87	    check();
    88	    for (const x of arms) for (const rec of x.records) expect([rec.residual, rec.matterResidual]).toEqual(["0", "0"]);
    89	  });
    90

     3	import { doseRow, noiseGate, probeW2, qualify, runLadder, wallGateBlockFe, wallPasses, type DoseRow, type WallSeedResult } from "../src/niche/dose.ts";

     7	const mk = (seed: number, v: number[]): WallSeedResult => ({ seed, contrasts: { builder: v[0], poly: v[1], space: v[2], inoc2: v[3] }, yWall: 0, viableReps: 2 });
     8	/** A synthetic dose row: `k` of 8 seeds positive on every contrast at the given effect. */
     9	const row = (totalP: number, k: number, effect: number): DoseRow => doseRow(totalP, Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => (i < k ? mk(i, [effect, effect, effect, effect]) : mk(i, [-effect, -effect, -effect, -effect]))));
    10	
    11	describe("positive-control dose ladder", () => {
    12	  it("ceiling derives from dev builder P per candidate (2 x median = the twice-median integer)", () => {
    13	    expect(qualify(0, 20, 207, [row(60, 8, 100)]).ceiling).toBe(207);
    14	    expect(qualify(4, 20, 169, [row(60, 8, 100)]).ceiling).toBe(169);
    15	  });
    16	
    17	  it("no dose above the ceiling can qualify; the minimum detectable dose is still stated", () => {
    18	    const q = qualify(0, 20, 170, [row(60, 3, 100), row(120, 4, 100), row(240, 5, 100), row(600, 8, 200)]);
    19	    expect(q.qualified).toBe(false);
    20	    expect(q.minDetectableDose).toBe(600);
    21	    expect(q.qualifyingDose).toBeNull();
    22	    expect(q.dosesWithinCeiling).toEqual([60, 120]);
    23	    expect(q.reason).toMatch(/minimum detectable dose 600 exceeds the realistic ceiling 170/);
    24	  });
    25	
    26	  it("wall pass rule: 7 of 8 positive on all four contrasts and median >= delta; G-NOISE needs delta <= half the limiting median", () => {
    27	    expect(wallPasses(row(120, 7, 100), 50)).toBe(true);
    28	    expect(wallPasses(row(120, 6, 100), 50)).toBe(false);
    29	    expect(wallPasses(row(120, 8, 40), 50)).toBe(false);
    30	    // 7 of 8 positive at +100 / -100: the median is +100.
    31	    expect(noiseGate(row(120, 7, 100), 50)).toBe(true);
    32	    expect(noiseGate(row(120, 7, 100), 51)).toBe(false);
    33	    const q = qualify(0, 60, 170, [row(120, 7, 100)]);
    34	    expect(q.qualified).toBe(false);
    35	    expect(q.reason).toMatch(/G-NOISE/);
    36	    expect(qualify(0, 50, 170, [row(120, 7, 100)]).qualifyingDose).toBe(120);
    37	  });
    38	
    39	  it("allowP wall never qualifies: the polymer floor is refused by ordinary placement, the ring is legal", () => {
    40	    const w2 = probeW2(PROTOCOL_FE);
    41	    expect(w2.status).toBe("unavailable");
    42	    expect(w2.reason).toMatch(/common placeable cells/);
    43	    const short: ProtocolFe = { ...PROTOCOL_FE, T2: 12, census: 1, R: 1 };
    44	    const r = wallGateBlockFe(short.wallSeeds[0], 120, short);
    45	    expect(Object.keys(r.contrasts).sort()).toEqual(["builder", "inoc2", "poly", "space"]);
    46	  }, 120_000);
    47	
    48	  it("the ladder is deterministic across reruns and stops once every candidate passes", () => {
    49	    const short: ProtocolFe = { ...PROTOCOL_FE, T2: 12, census: 1, R: 1, doses: [60, 120], wallSeeds: PROTOCOL_FE.wallSeeds.slice(0, 4) };
    50	    const a = runLadder({ "0": 0 }, short), b = runLadder({ "0": 0 }, short);
    51	    expect(canonicalJSON(a)).toBe(canonicalJSON(b));
    52	    // delta 0: any all-positive row passes, so a rerun that passed at 60 would stop there; either way rows are a prefix of the doses.
    53	    expect(a.rows.map((r) => r.totalP)).toEqual(short.doses.slice(0, a.rows.length));
    54	  }, 180_000);
    55	
    56	  it("failure yields the minimum detectable dose in a sealed stopped payload (no fresh seed simulated)", () => {
    57	    const rows = [row(60, 2, 10), row(120, 3, 10)];
    58	    const dev = { purpose: "calibration", R: 4, seeds: [], candidates: [], ladder: { geometry: "W1", rows, w2: { status: "unavailable", reason: "x" } }, qualification: PROTOCOL_FE.panel.map((c) => qualify(c, 100, 170, rows)) } as unknown as DevReportFe;
    59	    const p: ProtocolFe = { ...PROTOCOL_FE, delta: { "0": 100, "4": 100, "9": 100 } };
    60	    const fresh = freshReportFe(dev, p);
    61	    expect(fresh.ran).toBe(false);
    62	    expect(fresh.candidates).toEqual([]);
    63	    expect(fresh.results.every((r) => r.label === "unsupported-measurement" && !r.simulated)).toBe(true);
    64	    expect(fresh.panel.verdict).toBe("unsupported-measurement");
    65	    expect(fresh.panel.verdict).not.toMatch(/no-causal/);
    66	    expect(fresh.results[0].reasons.join(" ")).toMatch(/not detected at any ladder dose/);
    67	    const sealed = sealPayloadFe({ kind: "niche-fe-evidence", version: "t", protocol: p, protocolHash: "h", registryDigest: "d", seedCount: 0, stepProjection: { formula: "", freshBlocks: 0, stepsPerBlock: 0, devBlocks: 0 }, dev, fresh, fixtures: {} as never, powerStatement: p.powerStatement, limits: [], amendments: [] });
    68	    expect(verifySealFe(sealed)).toBe(true);
    69	    expect(verifySealFe({ ...sealed, limits: ["x"] })).toBe(false);
    70	  });

   130	    // k = 12 (p = 2517/65536) still clears the last Holm step (1/20) behind two perfect candidates; k = 11 (6885/65536) does not.
   131	    expect(decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 12), inputOf("c4", 16), inputOf("c9", 16)]).find((r) => r.id === "c0")!.label).toBe("supported");
   132	    const bad = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 11), inputOf("c4", 16), inputOf("c9", 16)]);

   134	  });
   135	
   136	  it("no floats in the sealed payload shape; power table values are exact (0.75: 40.5%, 0.9: 93.2%)", () => {
   137	    expect(() => canonicalJSON(decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16)]))).not.toThrow();
   138	    const pct = (qn: bigint, qd: bigint) => {
   139	      const t = binomialUpperTail(16, 13, qn, qd);
   140	      return Number((t.num * 10000n) / t.den) / 100;
   141	    };
   142	    expect(pct(3n, 4n)).toBeCloseTo(40.49, 1);
   143	    expect(pct(17n, 20n)).toBeCloseTo(78.98, 1);
   144	    expect(pct(9n, 10n)).toBeCloseTo(93.15, 1);
   145	    expect(pct(19n, 20n)).toBeCloseTo(99.29, 1);
   146	    expect(binomialUpperTail(16, 13, 1n, 2n).num * 65536n).toBe(697n * binomialUpperTail(16, 13, 1n, 2n).den);
   147	    expect(PROTOCOL_FE.powerStatement).toMatch(/40\.5%.*79%.*93%.*99%/);
   148	    expect(ALPHA).toEqual({ num: 1, den: 20 });
   149	  });
   150	
   151	  it("field arm viability requires 14 of 16 blocks; the paired survival contrast is not gating", () => {
   152	    const ok = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16, 16, { viableBlocks: 14 })])[0];
   153	    const no = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16, 16, { viableBlocks: 13 })])[0];
   154	    expect(ok.label).toBe("supported");
   155	    expect(no.label).toBe("unsupported-within-tested-domain");
   156	    expect(no.reasons.join(" ")).toMatch(/viable/);
   157	    expect(GATING_FE as readonly string[]).not.toContain("survival");
   158	  });
   159

    10	describe("second-rung fixtures", () => {
    11	  it("far-field-energy: naive positive, rung-1 D_inoc positive (fooled), D_inoc2 not positive, full gate unsupported via D_poly = 0 (caughtBy recorded)", () => {
    12	    const r = decideFixtureFe("far-field-energy", seeds, seeds.map((s) => fieldBlock("far-field", s, fp)), delta);
    13	    expect(r.naiveWouldSay).toBe("benefit");
    14	    expect(r.inocFooledOld).toBe(true);
    15	    expect(r.inocCaughtNew).toBe(true);
    16	    expect(r.kNew).toBeLessThan(r.kOld);
    17	    expect(r.gatingK.poly).toBe(0);
    18	    expect(r.caughtBy).toContain("poly");
    19	    expect(r.caughtBy).toContain("inoc2");
    20	    expect(r.fullGateLabel).not.toBe("supported");
    21	    // d0 (and so d1E, d1B) hold less free energy than the field; fU, fS, fB hold the same.
    22	    const free = (id: string) => BigInt(r.freeBefore.find((x) => x.id === id)!.freeE);
    23	    expect(free("d0")).toBeLessThan(free("b"));
    24	    for (const id of ["fU", "fS", "fB"]) expect(free(id)).toBe(free("b"));
    25	  }, 120_000);
    26	
    27	  it("free-gap-replay closes the free-E gap: d0 holds more free E at equal totals, fU/fS/fB reproduce b's free E exactly, every residual 0n", () => {
    28	    for (const s of seeds) {
    29	      const r = freeGapSeed(s, fp);
    30	      expect(r.d0HoldsMoreFree).toBe(true);
    31	      expect(r.totalsEqual).toBe(true);
    32	      for (const id of ["fU", "fS", "fB"]) {
    33	        expect(r.freeDiffBefore[id]).toBe("0");
    34	        expect(r.freeDiffAfter[id]).toBe("0");
    35	      }
    36	      // The field here binds its energy as detritus B only: fU and fS hold less total energy, and fB (which is handed that biomass as inoculum) closes the total gap too.
    37	      for (const id of ["d0", "fU", "fS"]) expect(id === "d0" ? r.constructionGap[id] : BigInt(r.constructionGap[id]) > 0n).toBeTruthy();
    38	      expect(r.flags.fU).toBe("comparator-deficit");
    39	      expect(r.constructionGap.fB).toBe("0");
    40	      expect(r.flags.fB).toBe("total-matched");
    41	      expect(r.residualsZero).toBe(true);
    42	    }
    43	  });
    44	
    45	  it("takeover-only still has zero synthesis; null-field gives exact zero contrasts", () => {
    46	    const t = takeoverBlockFe(seeds[0], fp);
    47	    expect(t.outcomes.b.synthesis).toBe(0);
    48	    const n = nullBlockFe(seeds[0], fp);
    49	    expect(n.contrasts).toMatchObject({ builder: 0, poly: 0, space: 0, inoc2: 0, naive: 0 });
    50	  }, 60_000);
    51	
    52	  it("hand-wall-gate-W2 (rung-1 allowP wall at the frozen scale) is a diagnostic through the second-rung arms", () => {
    53	    const p = { ...PROTOCOL_FE, R: PROTOCOL_FE.fixtureR };
    54	    const r = decideFixtureFe("hand-wall-gate-W2", seeds.slice(0, 4), seeds.slice(0, 4).map((s) => wallGateBlockFe2(s, p)), Math.ceil((p.reserve.B * p.R) / 50));
    55	    // Report what the gate returns; every contrast is computed and nothing throws. (The label is recorded in evidence, never qualifying.)
    56	    expect(Object.keys(r.gatingK).sort()).toEqual(["builder", "inoc2", "poly", "space"]);
    57	  }, 300_000);

     1	// I06b fixtures (PLAN section 4.5), 16x16 at unit scale. far-field-energy: a conditioned field that differs from its controls only by
     2	// extra free energy laid in a band OUTSIDE the inoculum footprint plus halo (no polymer). The first rung's inoculum contrast
     3	// (D_inoc: d1E, d1B only) can be fooled by it because those comparators read energy inside the region only; D_inoc2 adds fU/fS/fB.
     4	// Ablation only converts P to C, so with no polymer D_poly = 0 and the full gate rejects it: what is tested is the inoculum contrast.
     5	import { CH, cellCount, type Genome, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     6	import { disc, footprintCells, outsideRegion, placeAll, receiverAlone, regionCells, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     7	import { ALPHA, contrastResult, medianTimes2 } from "./decision.ts";
     8	import { decideCandidatesFe, type CandidateResultFe } from "./decisionFe.ts";
     9	import { buildFreeMatched, freeRows, fundBiomass, type FreeRow } from "./freeEnergy.ts";
    10	import { buildWall, controlsAround, nominalBudget, regionCellsExcludingFootprint, uniformBudget, wallCells, inertReceiver } from "./fixtures.ts";
    11	import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, type GatingFe, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
    12	import { contrastsOfFe, runArmReplicated, type ArmOutcomeFe, type ContrastsFe } from "./replicate.ts";
    13	import { NicheInvalid, audited, cloneAudit, cloneState, debitEven } from "./transfer.ts";
    14	
    15	export function fixtureProtocolFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): ProtocolFe {
    16	  return { ...p, grid: 16, T1: 0, T2: 40, injury: null, footprint: { x: 8, y: 8, radius: 2 }, halo: 2, nMin: 8, reserve: { B: 480, E: 24 }, nutrient: 100, R: p.fixtureR };
    17	}
    18	
    19	const setChannel = (s: WorldState, ch: number, cells: readonly number[], total: number): void => {
    20	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), m = cells.length, base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
    21	  cells.forEach((i, j) => (s.cells[ch * n + i] += base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0)));
    22	};
    23	
    24	export interface FeBlock {
    25	  outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>;
    26	  contrasts: ContrastsFe;
    27	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
    28	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
    29	  refused: { id: string; reason: string }[];
    30	}
    31	
    32	/** Controls around a hand-built conditioned arm `b`, plus fU/fS/fB; shared placement; R replicates of every arm. */
    33	export function runFeArms(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): FeBlock {
    34	  const arms = [...controlsAround(b, d0, seed, p)];
    35	  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], p);
    36	  if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("every free-energy-matched comparator was refused");
    37	  arms.push(...fm.arms);
    38	  const freeBefore = freeRows(arms);
    39	  placeAll(arms, p, opts);
    40	  const freeAfter = freeRows(arms);
    41	  const outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe> = {};
    42	  for (const x of arms) outcomes[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, p, p.R);
    43	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOfFe(outcomes), freeBefore, freeAfter, refused: fm.refused };
    44	}
    45	
    46	const condOf = (base: PreArm): PreArm => ({ id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] });
    47	
    48	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	// energy fields
    50	
    51	export type FieldVariant = "far-field" | "uniform" | "footprint";
    52	/** Extra stored free energy, sized so the naive metric clears the fixture delta. */
    53	export const FIELD_GIFT: Record<FieldVariant, number> = { "far-field": 10000, uniform: 10000, footprint: 4000 };
    54	
    55	/** Cells of the far-field band: outside the footprint-plus-halo region, within two more cells of radius. */
    56	export function bandCells(p: ProtocolFe): number[] {
    57	  const inR = new Set(regionCells(p));
    58	  return disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius + p.halo + 2, p.grid, p.grid).filter((i) => !inR.has(i));
    59	}
    60	
    61	export function fieldBlock(variant: FieldVariant, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, gift = FIELD_GIFT[variant]): FeBlock {
    62	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
    63	  const cond = condOf(base);
    64	  const cells = variant === "far-field" ? bandCells(p) : variant === "uniform" ? Array.from({ length: p.grid * p.grid }, (_, i) => i) : footprintCells(p);
    65	  setChannel(cond.state, CH.E, cells, gift);
    66	  cond.audit.energyStart += BigInt(gift);
    67	  return runFeArms(cond, base, seed, p);
    68	}
    69	
    70	/** Non-living detritus B on the halo ring (funded from A there and from E outside): bound energy instead of free, equal totals with receiver alone. An inert receiver is placed. */
    71	export function detritusField(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, detritus = 200): { base: PreArm; cond: PreArm } {
    72	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
    73	  const cond = condOf(base);
    74	  const ring = regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
    75	  audited("detritus", cond.state, cond.audit, () => {
    76	    const s = cond.state;
    77	    debitEven(s, CH.A, detritus, ring);
    78	    setChannel(s, CH.B, ring, detritus);
    79	    debitEven(s, CH.E, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * detritus, outsideRegion(p));
    80	    return { detritus };
    81	  });
    82	  return { base, cond };
    83	}
    84	export const takeoverBlockFe = (seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock => {
    85	  const { base, cond } = detritusField(seed, p);
    86	  return runFeArms(cond, base, seed, p, { genome: inertReceiver(p) });
    87	};
    88	export const nullBlockFe = (seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock => {
    89	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
    90	  return runFeArms(condOf(base), base, seed, p);
    91	};
    92	
    93	/** The rung-1 hand wall W2 (polymer floor under the receiver, allowP) at the frozen scale through the second-rung arms: a diagnostic, never qualifying. */
    94	export function wallGateBlockFe2(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock {
    95	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, p) }, seed, p);
    96	  const wall = condOf(base);
    97	  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W2", p), p.wall.totalP, p);
    98	  return runFeArms(wall, base, seed, p, { allowP: true });
    99	}
   100	
   101	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   102	// free-gap-replay
   103	
   104	export interface FreeGapSeed {
   105	  seed: number;
   106	  /** Rung-1 observed direction: receiver alone holds MORE free E than b with equal totals. */
   107	  d0FreeE: string;
   108	  bFreeE: string;
   109	  d0Total: string;
   110	  bTotal: string;
   111	  d0HoldsMoreFree: boolean;
   112	  totalsEqual: boolean;
   113	  /** Free-energy difference to b for each comparator, before and after placement (all zero). */
   114	  freeDiffBefore: Record<string, string>;
   115	  freeDiffAfter: Record<string, string>;
   116	  constructionGap: Record<string, string>;
   117	  flags: Record<string, string>;
   118	  residualsZero: boolean;
   119	}
   120	
   121	export function freeGapSeed(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FreeGapSeed {
   122	  const { base, cond } = detritusField(seed, p);
   123	  const b = runFeArmsPre(cond, base, seed, p);
   124	  const row = (rows: FreeRow[], id: string) => rows.find((r) => r.id === id)!;
   125	  const ids = ["fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => b.before.some((r) => r.id === id));
   126	  const d0 = row(b.before, "d0"), bb = row(b.before, "b");
   127	  return {
   128	    seed,
   129	    d0FreeE: d0.freeE,
   130	    bFreeE: bb.freeE,
   131	    d0Total: d0.total,
   132	    bTotal: bb.total,
   133	    d0HoldsMoreFree: BigInt(d0.freeE) > BigInt(bb.freeE),
   134	    totalsEqual: d0.total === bb.total,
   135	    freeDiffBefore: Object.fromEntries(ids.map((id) => [id, row(b.before, id).freeGapToB])),
   136	    freeDiffAfter: Object.fromEntries(ids.map((id) => [id, row(b.after, id).freeGapToB])),
   137	    constructionGap: Object.fromEntries(["d0", ...ids].map((id) => [id, row(b.before, id).constructionGap])),
   138	    flags: Object.fromEntries(["d0", ...ids].map((id) => [id, row(b.before, id).flag])),
   139	    residualsZero: b.residualsZero,
   140	  };
   141	}
   142	
   143	/** Arms and free-energy rows only, no Stage 2 (the replay is about construction). */
   144	function runFeArmsPre(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): { before: FreeRow[]; after: FreeRow[]; residualsZero: boolean } {
   145	  const arms = [...controlsAround(b, d0, seed, p)];
   146	  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], p);
   147	  arms.push(...fm.arms);
   148	  const before = freeRows(arms);
   149	  placeAll(arms, p, { genome: inertReceiver(p) });
   150	  const after = freeRows(arms);
   151	  const residualsZero = arms.every((x) => x.records.every((r) => r.residual === "0" && r.matterResidual === "0"));
   152	  return { before, after, residualsZero };
   153	}
   154	
   155	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   156	// guards
   157	
   158	export interface GuardFe {
   159	  id: string;
   160	  ok: boolean;
   161	  detail: string;
   162	}
   163	export function runGuardsFe(p: ProtocolFe): GuardFe[] {
   164	  const out: GuardFe[] = [];
   165	  const attempt = (id: string, fn: () => void, want: RegExp): void => {
   166	    try {
   167	      fn();
   168	      out.push({ id, ok: false, detail: "no refusal" });
   169	    } catch (e) {
   170	      out.push({ id, ok: e instanceof NicheInvalid && want.test(e.reason), detail: e instanceof NicheInvalid ? e.reason : String(e) });
   171	    }
   172	  };
   173	  attempt("credit-overdraw", () => {
   174	    const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, 1, p);
   175	    const f = buildFreeMatched(condOf(base), p).arms.find((x) => x.id === "fB")!;
   176	    fundBiomass(f, 10 ** 9, outsideRegion(p));
   177	  }, /credit-overdraw/);
   178	  return out;
   179	}
   180	
   181	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182	// fixture decisions
   183	
   184	export interface FixtureReportFe {
   185	  id: string;
   186	  seeds: readonly number[];
   187	  contrasts: ContrastsFe[];
   188	  naiveK: number;
   189	  naiveWouldSay: "benefit" | "no benefit";
   190	  /** The first rung's inoculum contrast alone passes its sign test with median >= delta: it is fooled. */
   191	  inocFooledOld: boolean;
   192	  /** The free-energy-matched inoculum contrast does not pass. */
   193	  inocCaughtNew: boolean;
   194	  kOld: number;
   195	  kNew: number;
   196	  gatingK: Record<GatingFe, number>;
   197	  /** Gating contrasts that fail (these reject the field in the full gate). */
   198	  caughtBy: GatingFe[];
   199	  fullGateLabel: CandidateResultFe["label"];
   200	  synthesis: number[];
   201	  /** Free energy of every arm for the first seed, before placement (the 9,917 vs 22,169 style table). */
   202	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
   203	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
   204	}
   205	
   206	export function decideFixtureFe(id: string, seeds: readonly number[], blocks: FeBlock[], delta: number): FixtureReportFe {
   207	  const cs = blocks.map((b) => b.contrasts);
   208	  const col = (k: keyof ContrastsFe) => cs.map((c) => c[k]);
   209	  const n = blocks.length;
   210	  const dec = decideCandidatesFe([{ id, status: "ok", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: col("builder"), poly: col("poly"), space: col("space"), inoc2: col("inoc2"), spaceP: col("spaceP"), spaceNP: col("spaceNP"), inoc: col("inoc"), naive: col("naive") }, delta, viableBlocks: n, blocks: n, expectedBlocks: n, notSimulatedReasons: [] }], 1)[0];
   211	  const old = contrastResult(col("inoc"), delta, ALPHA), nw = contrastResult(col("inoc2"), delta, ALPHA);
   212	  const naiveK = col("naive").filter((x) => x > 0).length;
   213	  const naivePositive = naiveK * 2 > n && medianTimes2(col("naive")) >= 2 * delta;
   214	  return {
   215	    id,
   216	    seeds,
   217	    contrasts: cs,
   218	    naiveK,
   219	    naiveWouldSay: naivePositive ? "benefit" : "no benefit",
   220	    inocFooledOld: old.passes,
   221	    inocCaughtNew: !nw.passes,
   222	    kOld: old.k,
   223	    kNew: nw.k,
   224	    gatingK: Object.fromEntries(GATING_FE.map((k) => [k, dec.gating[k].k])) as Record<GatingFe, number>,
   225	    caughtBy: GATING_FE.filter((k) => !dec.gating[k].passes),
   226	    fullGateLabel: dec.label,
   227	    synthesis: blocks.map((b) => b.outcomes.b?.synthesis ?? 0),
   228	    freeBefore: blocks[0].freeBefore,
   229	    freeAfter: blocks[0].freeAfter,
   230	  };
   231	}

    25	export const ARM_IDS = ["a", "b", "c", "cP", "cNP", "nb", "ab", "d0", "d1E", "d1B"] as const;
    26	export type ArmId = (typeof ARM_IDS)[number] | "rb" | "rnb" | "fU" | "fS" | "fB";

    28	export interface PreArm {
    29	  id: ArmId;
    30	  state: WorldState;
    31	  audit: Audit;
