    35	export const disc = (cx: number, cy: number, r: number, W: number, H: number): number[] => {
    36	  const out: number[] = [];
    37	  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) if ((x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2 <= r * r) out.push(y * W + x);
    38	  return out;
    39	};
    40	export const footprintCells = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] => disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius, p.grid, p.grid);
    41	/** Footprint plus halo: where inoculum-matching is measured. */
    42	export const regionCells = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] => disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius + p.halo, p.grid, p.grid);
    43	export const outsideRegion = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] => {
    44	  const inR = new Set(regionCells(p));
    45	  return Array.from({ length: p.grid * p.grid }, (_, i) => i).filter((i) => !inR.has(i));
    46	};
    47	
    48	export function startWorld(cand: Candidate, kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): WorldState {
    49	  const cfg = worldConfig(seed, p);
    50	  const f = p.founders;
    51	  const s = buildWorld(cfg, { nutrient: p.nutrient, founders: [{ x: f.x, y: f.y, radius: f.radius, genome: cand[kind], biomass: f.biomass, energy: f.energy }] });
    52	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    53	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[CH.E * n + i] += p.backgroundE;
    54	  return s;
    55	}
    56	
    57	export interface History {
    58	  state: WorldState;
    59	  audit: Audit;
    60	  records: TransferRecord[];
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** Stage 1: declared budget, reserve funded at step 0 and inert, T1 steps with the audit checked at every census. */
    64	export function runHistory(cand: Candidate, kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): History {
    65	  const state = startWorld(cand, kind, seed, p);
    66	  const audit = openAudit(state);
    67	  const records = [fundReserve(state, audit, p.reserve.B, p.reserve.E, outsideRegion(p))];
    68	  const sim = new RefSim(state);
    69	  for (let t = 1; t <= p.T1; t++) {
    70	    sim.step();
    71	    if (t % p.census === 0 || t === p.T1) assertAudit(sim.state, audit, `stage 1 step ${t}`);
    72	  }
    73	  return { state: sim.state, audit, records };
    74	}
    75	
    76	const sumChan = (s: WorldState, chs: readonly number[], idx: readonly number[]): number => {
    77	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    78	  let t = 0;
    79	  for (const ch of chs) for (const i of idx) t += s.cells[ch * n + i];
    80	  return t;
    81	};
    82	
    83	/**
    84	 * Receiver alone: the same total budget as uniform A and uniform E plus the reserve, nothing conditioned, at absolute step T1.
    85	 * `dissipation` (conditioned arms: heatOut - lightIn of the history, the net energy the conditioning stage released) is booked as
    86	 * named analytic heat and taken out of the uniform free E, so the comparator carries the same free-energy total as the conditioned
    87	 * field instead of a richer, never-dissipated one. Fixtures that have no history leave it zero.
    88	 */
    89	export interface Budget {
    90	  matterStart: bigint;
    91	  energyStart: bigint;
    92	}
    93	export function receiverAlone(h: { audit: Budget; dissipation?: bigint }, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): PreArm {
    94	  const cfg = worldConfig(seed, p);
    95	  const s = allocState(cfg);
    96	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    97	  const dissipation = h.dissipation ?? 0n;
    98	  const a: Audit = { energyStart: h.audit.energyStart, matterStart: h.audit.matterStart, reserveB: p.reserve.B, reserveE: p.reserve.E, heatAnalytic: dissipation };
    99	  if (a.energyStart - BigInt(cfg.eB) * BigInt(p.reserve.B) - BigInt(p.reserve.E) - dissipation < 0n) throw new NicheInvalid("receiver-alone: dissipation exceeds the free energy");
   100	  const spread = (ch: number, total: bigint) => {
   101	    const base = total / BigInt(n), rem = Number(total % BigInt(n));
   102	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[ch * n + i] = Number(base) + (i < rem ? 1 : 0);
   103	  };
   104	  spread(CH.A, a.matterStart - BigInt(p.reserve.B));
   105	  spread(CH.E, a.energyStart - BigInt(cfg.eB) * BigInt(p.reserve.B) - BigInt(p.reserve.E) - dissipation);
   106	  s.cells.fill(MOT_ZERO, CH.MOT * n, (CH.MOT + 1) * n);
   107	  s.step = p.T1;
   108	  assertAudit(s, a, "receiver-alone start");
   109	  return { id: "d0", state: s, audit: a, records: [] };
   110	}
   111	
   112	export interface BlockArms {
   113	  arms: PreArm[];
   114	  histories: { builder: History; nonbuilder: History };
   115	  dE: number;
   116	  dB: number;
   117	}
   118	
   119	/**
   120	 * The "more stored energy / more bound matter explains it" controls: the receiver-alone world with the reserve raised by what the
   121	 * conditioned arm `b` holds in the inoculum footprint plus halo beyond receiver-alone (taken from the lattice outside the region,
   122	 * debits recorded, totals unchanged). d1E raises free energy, d1B raises bound matter with its (eB - eA) chemical-energy debit.
   123	 */
   124	export function matchedControls(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): { d1E: PreArm; d1B: PreArm; dE: number; dB: number } {
   125	  const region = regionCells(p), outside = outsideRegion(p);
   126	  const dE = Math.max(0, sumChan(b.state, [CH.E], region) - sumChan(d0.state, [CH.E], region));
   127	  // Matched bound matter is B only: biomass, which a receiver can take over. (Summing A and C too would be vacuous: detritus that replaced nutrient on the same cells leaves the total unchanged. Adding P handed structural polymer to the receiver as living inoculum, so a pure polymer shield, 1200 P under the receiver, lost to the control by about 800 and the gate could never certify a structure; polymer is tested by the ablation and the scramble instead.)
   128	  const dB = Math.max(0, sumChan(b.state, [CH.B], region) - sumChan(d0.state, [CH.B], region));
   129	  const d1E: PreArm = { id: "d1E", state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
   130	  d1E.records.push(audited("matched-energy-debit", d1E.state, d1E.audit, () => (moveToReserve(d1E.state, d1E.audit, 0, dE, outside), { dE })));
   131	  const d1B: PreArm = { id: "d1B", state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
   132	  d1B.records.push(audited("matched-matter-debit", d1B.state, d1B.audit, () => (moveToReserve(d1B.state, d1B.audit, dB, (d1B.state.cfg.eB - d1B.state.cfg.eA) * dB, outside), { dB })));
   133	  return { d1E, d1B, dE, dB };
   134	}
   135	
   136	/** Build every pre-inoculum arm of one block (nothing placed yet). `replicates` adds the dev-only Stage-2 seed replicates. */
   137	export function buildArms(cand: Candidate, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, replicateSeed?: number): BlockArms {
   138	  const hb = runHistory(cand, "builder", seed, p);
   139	  const hn = runHistory(cand, "nonbuilder", seed, p);
   140	  if (hb.audit.energyStart !== hn.audit.energyStart || hb.audit.matterStart !== hn.audit.matterStart) throw new NicheInvalid("builder and nonbuilder histories start with different budgets");
   141	  const arm = (id: ArmId, h: History, ...ops: ((s: WorldState, a: Audit) => TransferRecord)[]): PreArm => {
   142	    const state = cloneState(h.state), audit = cloneAudit(h.audit);
   143	    return { id, state, audit, records: [...h.records, ...ops.map((f) => f(state, audit))] };
   144	  };
   145	  const mix = lowbias32;
   146	  const bRemoved = (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => relabelLiving(s, a);
   147	  const scr = (chs: number[], op: string) => (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => scrambleChannels(s, a, seed, chs, mix, op);
   148	  const ALL = [CH.A, CH.B, CH.C, CH.P, CH.E, CH.S];
   149	  const arms: PreArm[] = [
   150	    arm("a", hb),
   151	    arm("b", hb, bRemoved),
   152	    arm("c", hb, bRemoved, scr(ALL, "scramble")),
   153	    arm("cP", hb, bRemoved, scr([CH.P], "scramble-P")),
   154	    arm("cNP", hb, bRemoved, scr(ALL.filter((c) => c !== CH.P), "scramble-nonP")),
   155	    arm("nb", hn, bRemoved),
   156	    arm("ab", hb, bRemoved, convertPolymer),
   157	  ];
   158	  const d0 = receiverAlone({ audit: hb.audit, dissipation: hb.state.heatOut - hb.state.lightIn }, seed, p);
   159	  const { d1E, d1B, dE, dB } = matchedControls(arms[1], d0, p);
   160	  arms.push(d0, d1E, d1B);
   161	  if (replicateSeed !== undefined) {
   162	    const rep = (id: ArmId, src: PreArm): PreArm => ({ id, state: cloneState(src.state, { ...src.state.cfg, seed: replicateSeed }), audit: cloneAudit(src.audit), records: [...src.records] });
   163	    arms.push(rep("rb", arms[1]), rep("rnb", arms[5]));
   164	  }
   165	  return { arms, histories: { builder: hb, nonbuilder: hn }, dE, dB };
   166	}
   167	
   168	/** Place the same inoculum on the same cells in every arm: the intersection of placeable footprint cells. Needs at least nMin. */
   169	export function placeAll(arms: PreArm[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): number[] {
   170	  const cfg = arms[0].state.cfg;
   171	  const maxE = Math.max(...arms.map((x) => x.audit.reserveE));
   172	  const cells = commonCells(arms.map((x) => x.state), footprintCells(p), Math.ceil(maxE / p.nMin), opts.allowP ?? false);
   173	  if (cells.length < p.nMin) throw new NicheInvalid(`only ${cells.length} common placeable cells (< ${p.nMin})`);
   174	  const words = encodeGenome(opts.genome ?? receiverGenome(p), 0, packLineageLo(cfg, p.receiverRaw));
   175	  for (const x of arms) x.records.push(placeInoculum(x.state, x.audit, cells, words, opts.allowP ?? false));
   176	  return cells;
   177	}
   178	
     1	// Stage 2 and the readouts. Observers are read-only: they look at sim.state and sim.roles after each step and never write.
     2	import { CH, G, cellCount, packLineageLo, stateHash, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     3	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
     4	import { buildArms, placeAll, type ArmId, type BlockArms, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     5	import { PROTOCOL, candidateOf, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     6	import { NicheInvalid, assertAudit, snap, type Snap, type TransferRecord } from "./transfer.ts";
     7	
     8	export interface ArmOutcome {
     9	  id: ArmId;
    10	  /** Receiver-labelled biomass inventory B at the start of Stage 2 and at each census (last entry is T2). */
    11	  receiverB: number[];
    12	  /** Receiver-labelled B at T2: the primary endpoint Y. */
    13	  y: number;
    14	  survived: boolean;
    15	  builderB: number;
    16	  /** Receiver-cell PHOTO + GROW quanta summed over every Stage 2 step (synthesis), and DECOMP, RESP. null = unavailable: the co-culture arm (a) has no accurate producing-cell ownership (reactions are counted after the step, and injury can clear a lineage after it reacted), so it is never attributed. */
    17	  synthesis: number | null;
    18	  decomp: number | null;
    19	  resp: number | null;
    20	  /** A role counter reached its saturation value at some step: flux attribution is unavailable. */
    21	  saturated: boolean;
    22	  /** Y - B_r gain is at least half synthesis: "growth" may be said. Otherwise "inventory benefit, growth not attributed". */
    23	  growthAttributed: boolean;
    24	  energyEnd: string;
    25	  endHash: string;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export interface Hooks {
    29	  /** Called after each step with the state and the roles buffer (read only). */
    30	  onStep?: (state: WorldState, roles: Uint32Array, step: number) => void;
    31	}
    32	
    33	const lin = (s: WorldState, raw: number) => packLineageLo(s.cfg, raw);
    34	
    35	function receiverB(s: WorldState, raw: number): number {
    36	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    37	  const lo = lin(s, raw);
    38	  let t = 0;
    39	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === lo) t += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    40	  return t;
    41	}
    42	
    43	/** Run Stage 2 on a placed arm for T2 steps; throws NicheInvalid on any residual. */
    44	export function runArm(x: PreArm, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, hooks: Hooks = {}): ArmOutcome {
    45	  const sim = new RefSim(x.state);
    46	  const n = cellCount(x.state.cfg);
    47	  const blo = lin(x.state, p.builderRaw);
    48	  const y0 = receiverB(sim.state, p.receiverRaw);
    49	  const series = [y0];
    50	  let synthesis = 0, decomp = 0, resp = 0, saturated = false;
    51	  for (let t = 1; t <= p.T2; t++) {
    52	    sim.step();
    53	    const s = sim.state, roles = sim.roles;
    54	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    55	      const w0 = roles[2 * i], w1 = roles[2 * i + 1];
    56	      if ((w0 & 0xffff) >= p.roleSaturation || w0 >>> 16 >= p.roleSaturation || (w1 & 0xffff) >= p.roleSaturation || w1 >>> 16 >= p.roleSaturation) saturated = true;
    57	      // Roles are counted on every cell where the builder was removed (only receivers react there; injury may clear a lineage after it synthesized). The co-culture arm would need pre-reaction producing-cell ownership, which is not observed, so it is reported unavailable.
    58	      if (x.id !== "a") {
    59	        synthesis += (w0 & 0xffff) + (w0 >>> 16);
    60	        decomp += w1 & 0xffff;
    61	        resp += w1 >>> 16;
    62	      }
    63	    }
    64	    hooks.onStep?.(s, roles, s.step);
    65	    if (t % p.census === 0 || t === p.T2) {
    66	      assertAudit(s, x.audit, `stage 2 ${x.id} step ${t}`);
    67	      series.push(receiverB(s, p.receiverRaw));
    68	    }
    69	  }
    70	  const s = sim.state;
    71	  const errs = validateState(s);
    72	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`stage 2 ${x.id}: ${errs[0]}`);
    73	  let builderB = 0;
    74	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === blo) builderB += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    75	  const y = series[series.length - 1];
    76	  const gain = y - y0;
    77	  return {
    78	    id: x.id,
    79	    receiverB: series,
    80	    y,
    81	    survived: y > 0,
    82	    builderB,
    83	    synthesis: x.id === "a" ? null : synthesis,
    84	    decomp: x.id === "a" ? null : decomp,
    85	    resp: x.id === "a" ? null : resp,
    86	    saturated,
    87	    growthAttributed: x.id !== "a" && !saturated && gain > 0 && 2 * synthesis >= gain,
    88	    energyEnd: `${totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).energy}`,
    89	    endHash: stateHash(s),
    90	  };
    91	}
    92	
    93	export interface HistoryCost {
    94	  kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder";
    95	  B: string;
    96	  P: string;
    97	  E: string;
    98	  A: string;
    99	  C: string;
   100	  lightIn: string;
   101	  heatOut: string;
   102	  builderLineageB: number;
   103	  living: number;
   104	  /** kGene maintenance is not separable from catalytic and ordinary maintenance in react(); stated, not inferred. */
   105	  kGeneMaintenance: "unavailable";
   106	}
   107	
   108	export function historyCost(kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", s: WorldState, p: Protocol): HistoryCost {
   109	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
   110	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   111	  let b = 0, living = 0;
   112	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   113	    const hi = s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i], lo = s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i];
   114	    if ((hi | lo) !== 0) living++;
   115	    if (hi === 0 && lo === lin(s, p.builderRaw)) b += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
   116	  }
   117	  return { kind, B: `${t.B}`, P: `${t.P}`, E: `${t.E}`, A: `${t.A}`, C: `${t.C}`, lightIn: `${s.lightIn}`, heatOut: `${s.heatOut}`, builderLineageB: b, living, kGeneMaintenance: "unavailable" };
   118	}
   119	
   120	export interface BlockResult {
   121	  seed: number;
   122	  cluster: number;
   123	  valid: boolean;
   124	  invalidReason: string | null;
   125	  nPlaced: number;
   126	  dE: number;
   127	  dB: number;
   128	  costs: HistoryCost[];
   129	  startTotals: Record<string, Snap>;
   130	  transfers: Record<string, TransferRecord[]>;
   131	  outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>;
   132	  /** Integer contrasts (null when invalid). */
   133	  contrasts: Contrasts | null;
   134	}
   135	
   136	export interface Contrasts {
   137	  builder: number;
   138	  poly: number;
   139	  space: number;
   140	  inoc: number;
   141	  spaceP: number;
   142	  spaceNP: number;
   143	  naive: number;
   144	  /** Stage-2 replicate differences (dev only). */
   145	  replicateBuilder: number | null;
   146	  replicateNonbuilder: number | null;
   147	}
   148	
   149	export function contrastsOf(o: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>): Contrasts {
   150	  const y = (id: ArmId): number => {
   151	    const v = o[id];
   152	    if (!v) throw new NicheInvalid(`arm ${id} missing`);
   153	    return v.y;
   154	  };
   155	  const yb = y("b");
   156	  return {
   157	    builder: yb - y("nb"),
   158	    poly: yb - y("ab"),
   159	    space: yb - y("c"),
   160	    inoc: yb - Math.max(y("d1E"), y("d1B")),
   161	    spaceP: yb - y("cP"),
   162	    spaceNP: yb - y("cNP"),
   163	    naive: yb - y("d0"),
   164	    replicateBuilder: o.rb ? yb - o.rb.y : null,
   165	    replicateNonbuilder: o.rnb ? y("nb") - o.rnb.y : null,
   166	  };
   167	}
   168	
   169	/** One block: Stage 1 histories, transfers, shared placement, all Stage 2 arms. An invalid block is reported, never dropped. */
   170	export function runBlock(cluster: number, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, replicateSeed?: number, hooks: Hooks = {}): BlockResult {
   171	  const base: BlockResult = { seed, cluster, valid: false, invalidReason: null, nPlaced: 0, dE: 0, dB: 0, costs: [], startTotals: {}, transfers: {}, outcomes: {}, contrasts: null };
   172	  let built: BlockArms | null = null;
   173	  try {
   174	    built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p, replicateSeed);
   175	    base.dE = built.dE;
   176	    base.dB = built.dB;
   177	    base.costs = [historyCost("builder", built.histories.builder.state, p), historyCost("nonbuilder", built.histories.nonbuilder.state, p)];
   178	    base.nPlaced = placeAll(built.arms, p).length;
   179	    for (const x of built.arms) {
   180	      base.startTotals[x.id] = snap(x.state, x.audit);
   181	      base.transfers[x.id] = x.records;
   182	    }
   183	    for (const x of built.arms) base.outcomes[x.id] = runArm(x, p, hooks);
   184	    base.contrasts = contrastsOf(base.outcomes);
   185	    base.valid = true;
   186	  } catch (e) {
   187	    if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   188	    base.invalidReason = e.reason;
   189	    if (built) for (const x of built.arms) base.transfers[x.id] ??= x.records;
   190	  }
   191	  return base;
   192	}

     1	// I06 protocol: every number the assay depends on, frozen before the first fresh-seed run (docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/PLAN.md
     2	// sections 4 and 5). Dev (calibration) seeds may set the free parameters below; fresh (confirmation) seeds never tune anything.
     3	// Only integers and strings, so the protocol canonicalizes and hashes (canonicalJSON refuses floats).
     4	import { M3_FOUNDERS, canonicalJSON, defaultConfig, founderGenome, sha256HexJS, type Genome, type WorldConfig } from "@bl/schema";

     6	export const PROTOCOL = {
     7	  id: "niche-v1",
     8	  question: "builder->receiver dependency edge, first causal rung, mutation off",
     9	  grid: 24,
    10	  kernelRadius: 5,
    11	  genes: "ladder",
    12	  kGene: 200,
    13	  geneFlip: 0,
    14	  geneDrive: 40,
    15	  light: { mode: "uniform", base: 40, amp: 0 },
    16	  /** Recurring injury shared by every arm, or null (the calibration decides; absent keys otherwise). */
    17	  injury: { period: 10, radius: 2, prob: 22_000_000, shell: 15 } as null | { period: number; radius: number; prob: number; shell: number | null },
    18	  /** Conditioning and assay horizons, steps (each at most 120 so the regeneration budget holds). */
    19	  T1: 60,

    21	  /** Nutrient A per lattice cell and free energy E per cell at step 0 (the declared background of the initial budget). */
    22	  nutrient: 160,
    23	  backgroundE: 40,
    24	  founders: { x: 5, y: 12, radius: 2, biomass: 200, energy: 400 },
    25	  /** Receiver reserve R = (B, E) carried off-lattice from step 0 and inert until the transfer. */
    26	  reserve: { B: 480, E: 960 },
    27	  footprint: { x: 14, y: 12, radius: 3 },
    28	  halo: 2,
    29	  /** Bound matter of the biomass-matched control d1B is B in the footprint-plus-halo region (A + B + C + P would be vacuous for nutrient that detritus replaced on the same cells; B + P handed structural polymer over as living inoculum and beat a pure polymer wall, see REVIEW.md). Fixed before the amended fresh run. */
    30	  biomassMatch: "B",
    31	  nMin: 12,
    32	  /** Gene bytes: builder carries decomposition + build, nonbuilder and receiver decomposition only. */
    33	  geneBits: { builder: 3, nonbuilder: 1, receiver: 1 },
    34	  /** Raw lineage ids (hi = 0): the builder founder is 1; the receiver id is reserved and disjoint. */
    35	  builderRaw: 1,
    36	  receiverRaw: 2,
    37	  panel: [0, 4, 9] as readonly number[],
    38	  receiverCluster: 12,

    40	  roleSaturation: 65535,
    41	  census: 10,
    42	  /** Seeds: dev (calibration), fresh (confirmation), and the Stage-2 replicate seeds that give the delta floor on dev. All inside the registry range reserved for this slice. */
    43	  seedRange: { first: 8_600_001, last: 8_600_999 },
    44	  devSeeds: [8_600_001, 8_600_002, 8_600_003, 8_600_004] as readonly number[],
    45	  devReplicateSeeds: [8_600_201, 8_600_202, 8_600_203, 8_600_204] as readonly number[],
    46	  freshSeeds: [8_600_501, 8_600_502, 8_600_503, 8_600_504, 8_600_505, 8_600_506, 8_600_507, 8_600_508] as readonly number[],
    47	  /** Dev-only seeds of the hand-wall positive control (8, so an exact sign test can reach 9/256 <= 1/20). */
    48	  wallSeeds: [8_600_301, 8_600_302, 8_600_303, 8_600_304, 8_600_305, 8_600_306, 8_600_307, 8_600_308] as readonly number[],
    49	  /** Per-candidate delta from the dev stage-2 replicate control; filled in by --calibrate then frozen here. */
    50	  delta: { "0": 341, "4": 291, "9": 404 } as Readonly<Record<string, number>>,
    51	  /** Hand-built positive control. totalP is deliberately about ten times the dev builders' polymer at T1 (about 60 to 160), so the control can show the assay sees a structural effect at all; builderScaleP re-runs it at builder scale and is reported, never gating. */
    52	  wall: { totalP: 1200, builderScaleP: 120, ringGap: 1 },
    53	  /** PLAN 5.3: a candidate is adequately measured only when its dev builder polymer total at T1 (median over the dev blocks, compared as twice the median) exceeds that candidate's dev replicate floor delta (the Stage-2 noise floor, same integer units). Amended from a fixed 10 to match the PLAN text; see REVIEW.md. */
    54	  polymerAdequacy: "builder P median at T1 over dev blocks > candidate delta",
    55	};
    56	
    57	export type Protocol = typeof PROTOCOL;
    58	
    59	export interface Candidate {
    60	  cluster: number;
    61	  builder: Genome;
    62	  nonbuilder: Genome;
    63	}
    64	export const founderOf = (cluster: number) => {
    65	  const f = M3_FOUNDERS.find((x) => x.cluster === cluster);
    66	  if (!f) throw new Error(`no M3 founder for cluster ${cluster}`);
    67	  return f;
    68	};
    69	export const candidateOf = (cluster: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Candidate => {
    70	  const g = founderGenome(founderOf(cluster));
    71	  return { cluster, builder: { ...g, genes: p.geneBits.builder }, nonbuilder: { ...g, genes: p.geneBits.nonbuilder } };
    72	};
    73	export const receiverGenome = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Genome => ({ ...founderGenome(founderOf(p.receiverCluster)), genes: p.geneBits.receiver });
    74	
    75	export function worldConfig(seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): WorldConfig {
    76	  return defaultConfig({
    77	    tileW: p.grid,
    78	    tileH: p.grid,
    79	    kernelRadius: p.kernelRadius,
    80	    seed,
    81	    mutRate: 0,
    82	    genes: p.genes as "ladder",
    83	    kGene: p.kGene,
    84	    geneFlip: p.geneFlip,
    85	    geneDrive: p.geneDrive,
    86	    lightMode: p.light.mode as "uniform",
    87	    lightBase: p.light.base,
    88	    lightAmp: p.light.amp,
    89	    ...(p.injury ? { injuryPeriod: p.injury.period, injuryRadius: p.injury.radius, injuryProb: p.injury.prob, ...(p.injury.shell ? { injuryShell: p.injury.shell } : {}) } : {}),
    90	  });
    91	}
    92	
    93	/** SHA-256 of the canonical protocol plus the seed-registry digest it was checked against. */
    94	export function protocolHash(registryDigest: string, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): string {
    95	  return sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON({ protocol: p, registryDigest })));
    96	}

     1	// Exact, audited transfers between the conditioning stage and the assay stage. Every operation is analytical (outside the step
     2	// function), integer, and checked against the budget invariant: lattice + off-lattice reserve + cumulative analytic heat.
     3	//   energy:  lattice.energy + heatOut + heatAnalytic + reserveEnergy  ==  energyStart + lightIn
     4	//   matter:  lattice.matter + reserveB                                ==  matterStart
     5	// `ledgerResidual` counts neither the reserve nor heatAnalytic, so the wrapper below is the audited quantity.
     6	import { CELL_CHANNELS, CH, G, GENOME_CHANNELS, MOT_ZERO, POOL_MAX, cellCount, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldConfig, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     7	
     8	export class NicheInvalid extends Error {
     9	  reason: string;
    10	  constructor(reason: string) {
    11	    super(reason);
    12	    this.reason = reason;
    13	  }
    14	}
    15	
    16	export interface Audit {
    17	  energyStart: bigint;
    18	  matterStart: bigint;
    19	  /** Receiver reserve still off the lattice: B valued at eB per quantum, E at one. */
    20	  reserveB: number;
    21	  reserveE: number;
    22	  /** Cumulative energy released by analytical conversions, booked as heat. */
    23	  heatAnalytic: bigint;
    24	}
    25	
    26	export const reserveEnergy = (cfg: WorldConfig, a: Audit): bigint => BigInt(cfg.eB) * BigInt(a.reserveB) + BigInt(a.reserveE);
    27	
    28	export function residuals(s: WorldState, a: Audit): { energy: bigint; matter: bigint } {
    29	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
    30	  return {
    31	    energy: t.energy + s.heatOut + a.heatAnalytic + reserveEnergy(s.cfg, a) - a.energyStart - s.lightIn,
    32	    matter: t.matter + BigInt(a.reserveB) - a.matterStart,
    33	  };
    34	}
    35	
    36	export function assertAudit(s: WorldState, a: Audit, where: string): void {
    37	  const r = residuals(s, a);
    38	  if (r.energy !== 0n || r.matter !== 0n) throw new NicheInvalid(`${where}: nonzero residual (energy ${r.energy}, matter ${r.matter})`);
    39	}
    40	
    41	export function cloneState(s: WorldState, cfg: WorldConfig = s.cfg): WorldState {
    42	  return { cfg, step: s.step, cells: s.cells.slice(), genome: s.genome.slice(), lightIn: s.lightIn, heatOut: s.heatOut, flux: s.flux.slice() };
    43	}
    44	export const cloneAudit = (a: Audit): Audit => ({ ...a });
    45	
    46	export interface Snap {
    47	  A: string;
    48	  B: string;
    49	  C: string;
    50	  P: string;
    51	  E: string;
    52	  S: string;
    53	  matter: string;
    54	  energy: string;
    55	  reserveB: number;
    56	  reserveE: number;
    57	  heatAnalytic: string;
    58	  living: number;
    59	}
    60	export function livingCount(s: WorldState): number {
    61	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    62	  let k = 0;
    63	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if ((s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0) k++;
    64	  return k;
    65	}
    66	export function snap(s: WorldState, a: Audit): Snap {
    67	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
    68	  return { A: `${t.A}`, B: `${t.B}`, C: `${t.C}`, P: `${t.P}`, E: `${t.E}`, S: `${t.S}`, matter: `${t.matter}`, energy: `${t.energy}`, reserveB: a.reserveB, reserveE: a.reserveE, heatAnalytic: `${a.heatAnalytic}`, living: livingCount(s) };
    69	}
    70	
    71	export interface TransferRecord {
    72	  op: string;
    73	  before: Snap;
    74	  after: Snap;
    75	  detail: Record<string, number | string>;
    76	  /** Both exactly "0" for a valid record. */
    77	  residual: string;
    78	  matterResidual: string;
    79	}
    80	
    81	/** Run one mutation of (s, a) and record before/after; a nonzero residual or a failed validateState throws. */
    82	export function audited(op: string, s: WorldState, a: Audit, fn: () => Record<string, number | string>): TransferRecord {
    83	  assertAudit(s, a, `${op} (before)`);
    84	  const before = snap(s, a);
    85	  const detail = fn();
    86	  const errs = validateState(s);
    87	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`${op}: validateState: ${errs[0]}`);
    88	  const r = residuals(s, a);
    89	  if (r.energy !== 0n || r.matter !== 0n) throw new NicheInvalid(`${op}: nonzero residual (energy ${r.energy}, matter ${r.matter})`);
    90	  return { op, before, after: snap(s, a), detail, residual: `${r.energy}`, matterResidual: `${r.matter}` };
    91	}
    92	
    93	/** Split `total` evenly over `cells` (floor share, remainder to the first cells in order) and take it from channel `ch`. */
    94	export function debitEven(s: WorldState, ch: number, total: number, cells: readonly number[]): void {
    95	  if (total === 0) return;
    96	  const m = cells.length;
    97	  if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell can fund the reserve");
    98	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    99	  const base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
   100	  for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) {
   101	    const share = base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0);
   102	    if (s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]] < share) throw new NicheInvalid(`underfunded: cell ${cells[j]} holds ${s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]]} < ${share} of channel ${ch}`);
   103	  }
   104	  for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]] -= base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0);
   105	}
   106	
   107	/**
   108	 * Move `dB` quanta of lattice nutrient (matter) and `dEnergy` units of lattice free energy into the reserve. The reserve gains
   109	 * B = dB (valued eB each) and E = dEnergy - (eB - eA) * dB, so the funded reserve energy is exactly what left the lattice.
   110	 */
   111	export function moveToReserve(s: WorldState, a: Audit, dB: number, dEnergy: number, from: readonly number[]): void {
   112	  const { eA, eB } = s.cfg;
   113	  const e = dEnergy - (eB - eA) * dB;
   114	  if (e < 0) throw new NicheInvalid("reserve energy would be negative");
   115	  debitEven(s, CH.A, dB, from);
   116	  debitEven(s, CH.E, dEnergy, from);
   117	  a.reserveB += dB;
   118	  a.reserveE += e;
   119	}
   120	
   121	/** Start an audit for `s` as it is (nothing off the lattice yet). */
   122	export function openAudit(s: WorldState): Audit {
   123	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
   124	  return { energyStart: t.energy + s.heatOut - s.lightIn, matterStart: t.matter, reserveB: 0, reserveE: 0, heatAnalytic: 0n };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** Fund the reserve (Br, Er) from the lattice: A down by Br, E down by (eB - eA) * Br + Er, over `from`. */
   128	export function fundReserve(s: WorldState, a: Audit, Br: number, Er: number, from: readonly number[]): TransferRecord {
   129	  return audited("fund-reserve", s, a, () => {
   130	    moveToReserve(s, a, Br, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * Br + Er, from);
   131	    return { reserveB: Br, reserveE: Er, cells: from.length };
   132	  });
   133	}
   134	
   135	const livingAt = (s: WorldState, i: number): boolean => {
   136	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   137	  return (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0;
   138	};
   139	
   140	/** Builder removal: living cells become non-living (lineage and genome cleared); B, P, E, A, C, S stay in place. */
   141	export function relabelLiving(s: WorldState, a: Audit): TransferRecord {
   142	  return audited("relabel-living", s, a, () => {
   143	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   144	    let cells = 0, B = 0;
   145	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   146	      if (!livingAt(s, i)) continue;
   147	      cells++;
   148	      B += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
   149	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = 0;
   150	      s.cells[CH.MOT * n + i] = MOT_ZERO;
   151	    }
   152	    return { cells, relabelledB: B };
   153	  });
   154	}
   155	
   156	/** Ablation: every structural polymer quantum becomes waste C; the released energy (eP - eC) * P is booked as heatAnalytic. */
   157	export function convertPolymer(s: WorldState, a: Audit): TransferRecord {
   158	  return audited("polymer-to-waste", s, a, () => {
   159	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   160	    let P = 0;
   161	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   162	      const p = s.cells[CH.P * n + i];
   163	      P += p;
   164	      s.cells[CH.C * n + i] += p;
   165	      s.cells[CH.P * n + i] = 0;
   166	    }
   167	    a.heatAnalytic += BigInt(s.cfg.eP - s.cfg.eC) * BigInt(P);
   168	    return { convertedP: P, heatAnalytic: `${BigInt(s.cfg.eP - s.cfg.eC) * BigInt(P)}` };
   169	  });
   170	}
   171	
   172	/** Deterministic permutation of 0..n-1 from (seed, channel) alone, never from cell contents, so every history is scrambled the same way. */
   173	export function permutation(n: number, seed: number, ch: number, mix: (x: number) => number): number[] {
   174	  const p = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i);
   175	  for (let j = n - 1; j > 0; j--) {
   176	    const r = mix((seed ^ Math.imul(ch + 1, 0x9e3779b1) ^ Math.imul(j + 1, 0x85ebca6b)) >>> 0) % (j + 1);
   177	    const t = p[j];
   178	    p[j] = p[r];
   179	    p[r] = t;
   180	  }
   181	  return p;
   182	}
   183	
   184	/** Independently permute the listed channels over all lattice cells; each channel total is preserved exactly, structure is not. */
   185	export function scrambleChannels(s: WorldState, a: Audit, seed: number, channels: readonly number[], mix: (x: number) => number, op = "scramble"): TransferRecord {
   186	  return audited(op, s, a, () => {
   187	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   188	    for (const ch of channels) {
   189	      const p = permutation(n, seed, ch, mix);
   190	      const old = s.cells.slice(ch * n, (ch + 1) * n);
   191	      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[ch * n + i] = old[p[i]];
   192	    }
   193	    return { channels: channels.join(",") };
   194	  });
   195	}
   196	
   197	/** A cell that may take receiver inoculum: nothing bound, no lineage, and room in E. `allowP` is for the hand-wall floor only. */
   198	export function placeable(s: WorldState, i: number, perE: number, allowP = false): boolean {
   199	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   200	  return s.cells[CH.B * n + i] === 0 && (allowP || s.cells[CH.P * n + i] === 0) && !livingAt(s, i) && s.cells[CH.E * n + i] + perE <= POOL_MAX;
   201	}
   202	
   203	/** Cells that are placeable in every state of the block (the intersection), in raster order. */
   204	export function commonCells(states: readonly WorldState[], disc: readonly number[], perE: number, allowP = false): number[] {
   205	  return disc.filter((i) => states.every((s) => placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)));
   206	}
   207	
   208	/** Move the whole reserve onto `cells` (even split, remainder to the first): empty cells only, nothing overwritten. */
   209	export function placeInoculum(s: WorldState, a: Audit, cells: readonly number[], words: Uint32Array, allowP = false): TransferRecord {
   210	  return audited("place-inoculum", s, a, () => {
   211	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   212	    const m = cells.length;
   213	    if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell to place the inoculum on");
   214	    if (a.reserveB === 0 && a.reserveE === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("reserve exhausted: nothing left to place");
   215	    const bBase = Math.floor(a.reserveB / m), bRem = a.reserveB % m;
   216	    const eBase = Math.floor(a.reserveE / m), eRem = a.reserveE % m;
   217	    const perE = eBase + (eRem > 0 ? 1 : 0);
   218	    for (const i of cells) if (!placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)) throw new NicheInvalid(`placement refused: cell ${i} is occupied or at its pool cap`);
   219	    const B = a.reserveB, E = a.reserveE;
   220	    cells.forEach((i, j) => {
   221	      s.cells[CH.B * n + i] += bBase + (j < bRem ? 1 : 0);
   222	      s.cells[CH.E * n + i] += eBase + (j < eRem ? 1 : 0);
   223	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = words[g];
   224	    });
   225	    a.reserveB = 0;
   226	    a.reserveE = 0;
   227	    return { placedCells: m, placedB: B, placedE: E };
   228	  });
   229	}
   230	
     1	// Fixtures: the negative case that fools the naive metric (energy-gift), the positive control (hand-wall), the relabelling trap
     2	// (takeover-only), a null field, and the guard checks. Unit-scale fixtures use 16x16 worlds with kernelRadius 5 (schema validation
     3	// needs dimensions divisible by eight and 2*kernelRadius+1 <= tile); the positive control is also qualified at the frozen scale.
     4	import { CH, cellCount, encodeGenome, founderGenome, lowbias32, packLineageLo, type Genome, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { disc, footprintCells, matchedControls, outsideRegion, placeAll, receiverAlone, regionCells, startWorld, type ArmId, type Budget, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     6	import { contrastsOf, runArm, type ArmOutcome, type Contrasts } from "./assay.ts";
     7	import { decideCandidates, medianTimes2, type CandidateResult } from "./decision.ts";
     8	import { PROTOCOL, candidateOf, founderOf, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     9	import { NicheInvalid, audited, cloneAudit, cloneState, convertPolymer, debitEven, openAudit, placeInoculum, scrambleChannels, type Audit } from "./transfer.ts";
    10	
    11	export const FIXTURE_SEEDS: readonly number[] = [8_600_401, 8_600_402, 8_600_403, 8_600_404, 8_600_405, 8_600_406, 8_600_407, 8_600_408];
    12	
    13	export function fixtureProtocol(): Protocol {
    14	  return { ...PROTOCOL, grid: 16, T1: 0, T2: 40, injury: null, footprint: { x: 8, y: 8, radius: 2 }, halo: 2, nMin: 8, reserve: { B: 480, E: 24 }, nutrient: 100 };
    15	}
    16	
    17	/** Uniform-A / uniform-E budget of a w x w lattice with the reserve inside it (no founders). */
    18	export const uniformBudget = (p: Protocol): Budget => {
    19	  const n = p.grid * p.grid;
    20	  return { matterStart: BigInt(p.nutrient * n), energyStart: BigInt(p.backgroundE * n + 10 * p.reserve.B + p.reserve.E) };
    21	};
    22	
    23	const setChannel = (s: WorldState, ch: number, cells: readonly number[], total: number): void => {
    24	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), m = cells.length, base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
    25	  cells.forEach((i, j) => (s.cells[ch * n + i] += base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0)));
    26	};
    27	
    28	/** Run all pre-inoculum arms of a fixture block through the shared placement and Stage 2. */
    29	export function runPre(arms: PreArm[], p: Protocol, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>> {
    30	  placeAll(arms, p, opts);
    31	  const out: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>> = {};
    32	  for (const x of arms) out[x.id] = runArm(x, p);
    33	  return out;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Controls around a conditioned pre-arm `b` (any hand-built field): scrambles, ablation, receiver-alone and matched-inventory. */
    37	export function controlsAround(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: Protocol): PreArm[] {
    38	  const mk = (id: ArmId, ...ops: ((s: WorldState, a: Audit) => unknown)[]): PreArm => {
    39	    const state = cloneState(b.state), audit = cloneAudit(b.audit);
    40	    ops.forEach((f) => f(state, audit));
    41	    return { id, state, audit, records: [] };
    42	  };
    43	  const ALL = [CH.A, CH.B, CH.C, CH.P, CH.E, CH.S];
    44	  const sc = (chs: number[]) => (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => scrambleChannels(s, a, seed, chs, lowbias32);
    45	  const { d1E, d1B } = matchedControls(b, d0, p);
    46	  const nb = { id: "nb" as ArmId, state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
    47	  return [
    48	    { ...b, id: "b" },
    49	    mk("c", sc(ALL)),
    50	    mk("cP", sc([CH.P])),
    51	    mk("cNP", sc(ALL.filter((c) => c !== CH.P))),
    52	    mk("ab", (s, a) => convertPolymer(s, a)),
    53	    nb,
    54	    d0,
    55	    d1E,
    56	    d1B,
    57	  ];
    58	}
    59	
    60	/** Halo ring cells: in the footprint-plus-halo region but not in the footprint. */
    61	export const regionCellsExcludingFootprint = (p: Protocol): number[] => {
    62	  const fp = new Set(footprintCells(p));
    63	  return regionCells(p).filter((i) => !fp.has(i));
    64	};
    65	
    66	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67	// energy-gift
    68	
    69	export type GiftVariant = "uniform" | "footprint" | "annulus";
    70	/** Extra stored free energy per variant, sized so the naive metric clears the fixture delta in each. */
    71	export const GIFT: Record<GiftVariant, number> = { uniform: 10000, footprint: 4000, annulus: 4000 };
    72	
    73	/** A world that differs from receiver-alone only by extra stored free energy, declared in its budget (it is a different budget on purpose). */
    74	export function giftBlock(variant: GiftVariant, seed: number, p: Protocol = fixtureProtocol(), gift = GIFT[variant]): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts; gift: number } {
    75	  const cfgSeed = seed;
    76	  const budget = uniformBudget(p);
    77	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: budget }, cfgSeed, p);
    78	  const cond: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
    79	  const W = p.grid;
    80	  const cells = variant === "uniform" ? Array.from({ length: W * W }, (_, i) => i) : variant === "footprint" ? footprintCells(p) : regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
    81	  setChannel(cond.state, CH.E, cells, gift);
    82	  cond.audit.energyStart += BigInt(gift);
    83	  const arms = controlsAround(cond, base, seed, p);
    84	  const outcomes = runPre(arms, p);
    85	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOf(outcomes), gift };
    86	}
    87	
    88	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89	// hand-wall
    90	
    91	export type WallKind = "W1" | "W2";
    92	
    93	/** W1: a polymer ring around the footprint (gates A/C diffusion). W2: a polymer floor under the footprint cells (a local-P shield under injuryShell). */
    94	export function wallCells(kind: WallKind, p: Protocol): number[] {
    95	  const f = p.footprint;
    96	  if (kind === "W2") return footprintCells(p);
    97	  const inner = new Set(disc(f.x, f.y, f.radius + p.wall.ringGap - 1, p.grid, p.grid));
    98	  return disc(f.x, f.y, f.radius + p.wall.ringGap + 1, p.grid, p.grid).filter((i) => !inner.has(i));
    99	}
   100	
   101	/** Build P on `cells` out of lattice nutrient on the same cells and pay (eP - eA) * P from lattice E outside the region. */
   102	export function buildWall(x: PreArm, cells: readonly number[], totalP: number, p: Protocol): void {
   103	  audited("build-wall", x.state, x.audit, () => {
   104	    const s = x.state;
   105	    debitEven(s, CH.A, totalP, cells);
   106	    setChannel(s, CH.P, cells, totalP);
   107	    debitEven(s, CH.E, (s.cfg.eP - s.cfg.eA) * totalP, outsideRegion(p));
   108	    return { totalP, cells: cells.length };
   109	  });
   110	}
   111	
   112	/** One hand-wall block: the wall against the same P inventory scrambled. D = Y(wall) - Y(P scrambled). */
   113	export function wallBlock(kind: WallKind, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, budget?: Budget): { d: number; wall: ArmOutcome; scrambled: ArmOutcome } {
   114	  const bud = budget ?? nominalBudget(seed, p);
   115	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: bud }, seed, p);
   116	  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
   117	  buildWall(wall, wallCells(kind, p), p.wall.totalP, p);
   118	  const scr: PreArm = { id: "cP", state: cloneState(wall.state), audit: cloneAudit(wall.audit), records: [] };
   119	  scrambleChannels(scr.state, scr.audit, seed, [CH.P], lowbias32, "scramble-P");
   120	  const out = runPre([wall, scr], p, { allowP: true });
   121	  return { d: out.b!.y - out.cP!.y, wall: out.b!, scrambled: out.cP! };
   122	}
   123	
   124	/**
   125	 * The wall field pushed through every arm and all four gating contrasts, the way a conditioned field would be. The "nonbuilder"
   126	 * is the receiver alone (there is no history). Placement allows polymer under the receiver (allowP): that is outside the real
   127	 * placement rule, so this shows what the gate returns for a hand-built structure, not for a builder's field.
   128	 */
   129	export function wallGateBlock(kind: WallKind, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts } {
   130	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, p) }, seed, p);
   131	  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
   132	  buildWall(wall, wallCells(kind, p), p.wall.totalP, p);
   133	  const outcomes = runPre(controlsAround(wall, base, seed, p), p, { allowP: true });
   134	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOf(outcomes) };
   135	}
   136	
   137	/** The declared initial budget of a real block at this seed (founders included), without running anything. */
   138	export function nominalBudget(seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Budget {
   139	  const a = openAudit(startWorld(candidateOf(p.panel[0], p), "builder", seed, p));
   140	  return { matterStart: a.matterStart, energyStart: a.energyStart };
   141	}
   142	
   143	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144	// takeover-only
   145	
   146	/** A receiver that cannot synthesize anything: zero controller weights, no gene bits. */
   147	export const inertReceiver = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Genome => ({ ...founderGenome(founderOf(p.receiverCluster)), weights: new Int8Array(160), genes: 0 });
   148	
   149	/** Non-living detritus B on the halo ring next to the footprint (funded from A there and from E elsewhere); an inert receiver is placed. */
   150	export function takeoverBlock(seed: number, p: Protocol = fixtureProtocol(), detritus = 200): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts } {

docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:19:1. **Evidence fixtures with a negative case that fools the old inoculum contrast** (section 4.5). New fixture `far-field-energy`: a conditioned field that differs from its controls only by extra free energy laid in a band outside the inoculum footprint plus halo (no polymer). Because ablation only converts P to C (`transfer.ts`), with no polymer `b` and `ab` stay identical and `D_poly = 0`, so the complete rung-1 gate and the new gate both reject it. What the fixture tests is the **inoculum contrast alone**: rung-1 `D_inoc` (vs d0/d1E/d1B/region-matched) reads positive (fooled) while `D_inoc2` (with fU/fS/fB) does not. Evidence records `fullGateLabel` (unsupported, by `D_poly`) and `inocFooledOld` / `inocCaughtNew` separately; the claim made is about the contrast, never "the old rule would label it supported". A full-gate trap needs a polymer-bearing confound and is deferred (section 11). If the fixture cannot fool `D_inoc` at unit scale, the test fails and the slice reports that instead of weakening the claim.

docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:28:- **Registry binding.** Rung 1 hashes the whole registry (minus owners listed in `LATER_SLICE_OWNERS`). This rung adds the owner prefix `i06b free-energy-matched` to that list (the I08 precedent) and binds its own digest over its own reservation only, so neither rung's evidence is invalidated by the other's reservation.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:29:- **Audit.** `transfer.ts` `Audit`/`residuals` have no credit term and `runArm` audits every census. This rung adds **no credit to the audit**: each arm's ledger baseline is that arm's own constructed starting total (lattice + reserve, heatAnalytic 0), so each arm's physical residual is exactly `0n` as in rung 1. The construction difference between `b` and a comparator is a comparison-budget `constructionGap` recorded in evidence only, not a ledger entry (it is not energy created inside any run). If the existing audit cannot take a per-arm baseline, add an optional `baseline` parameter defaulting to current behaviour and prove rung-1 serialization unchanged by test.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:38:### 4.1 Free-energy-matched comparators (answers defect 1)
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:40:Free energy means lattice channel E (`sum E`), reported next to total energy (`totalsOf`), for the builder-removed arm `b` and each comparator, at the start of Stage 2 (before inoculum placement) and after placement. Matter is identical in every arm (lattice plus reserve), as in rung 1. Observed rung-1 direction (seed 8600501, cluster 0): the receiver-alone control d0 held MORE free E than `b` (22,169 vs 9,917) with equal total (26,969), because `b` holds its energy as bound matter.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:42:Matching free energy and total energy simultaneously is impossible at equal matter when the comparator has no conditioned composition (bound matter carries energy at eB, eP, eC, eA). Each comparator is a separately constructed initial state (not a transformation of `b` inside a run); its own ledger starts from its own total and conserves exactly. The difference is reported as `constructionGap = totalEnergy(b) - totalEnergy(f)` (signed BigInt, evidence only, never in the ledger): **gap > 0** means the comparator holds less total energy than the field arm; **gap < 0** means it holds more (a stronger comparator, the conservative side for the claim).
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:45:- **fS (spatial free-matched):** as fU but E copied cell for cell from `b` (same free-energy field, no polymer, no biomass, matter all A in the remainder). Isolates "the energy pattern alone" from "structure".
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:46:- **fB (free-matched plus biomass-matched):** fS plus the rung-1 d1B raise of B (region plus halo, matter converted from A), **without** the `(eB - eA)*dB` lattice debit, so free E stays equal to `b` cell for cell before and after placement; the bound-energy increase is entered as extra total energy (gap < 0, flagged `comparator-excess`). Test: free-E equality before and after placement.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:49:New inoculum contrast: `D_inoc2 = Y(b | builder) - max(Y(d1E), Y(d1B), Y(fU), Y(fS), Y(fB))`; every constructed comparator is in the max (none excluded by sign of gap; `comparator-excess` and `comparator-deficit` are labels in evidence). Rung-1 `D_inoc` is still computed and reported next to it. The four gating contrasts are builder, poly, space, inoc2. A comparator whose construction would need negative lattice E or exceed `POOL_MAX`/`MATTER_MAX` is refused (`credit-overdraw`, never clamped); a block where fU, fS and fB are all refused is `invalid-or-incomplete` for that candidate (reported, never dropped).
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:51:### 4.2 Better-powered readout (answers defect 2)
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:56:- **Power statement (frozen wording).** The first Holm rejection needs k >= 13 of 16 (p = 697/65,536). A contrast whose true per-block positive probability is 0.75 passes it only 40.5% of the time, 0.85 gives 79%, 0.90 gives 93%, 0.95 gives 99%. The design therefore detects consistent benefits (about 0.9 or higher of blocks positive and median above delta); a negative means "no benefit of size at least delta in at least about 90% of blocks", not "no benefit". Four contrasts, delta and viability can only lower the pass rate. Dev reports the empirical positive-block rate of the wall control at the qualifying dose next to this table.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:59:- **Noise gate (G-NOISE, dev only, before any fresh seed), per candidate:** `delta_c` must be at most half of the wall control's median effect on the **limiting gating contrast** (the one with the smallest median effect among the four), in summed-R units, at that candidate's qualifying dose (4.3). Otherwise the rung stops `unsupported-measurement` and reports the achieved floor and the minimum detectable dose; the fresh seeds are not read.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:66:- **Ordinary placement required.** The rung-1 `wallGateBlock` uses `allowP: true` (overwriting polymer on injured cells), which real transfers (`placeAll` default false) forbid. The qualifying control must place the wall by the ordinary placement rule (no overwrite of existing polymer, same pool-cap and reserve guards) at cells where that is legal; wall variants that need `allowP: true` stay as a reported diagnostic and never qualify the assay. If no ordinary-placement wall geometry exists, the control is `unavailable` and the stated reason is that none exists, ending the rung `unsupported-measurement`.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:68:- A candidate that does not qualify ends `unsupported-measurement` with the minimum detectable dose, its ratio to the builder polymer and the floor that explains it; its fresh blocks are not simulated (a sealed stopped payload is still written, section 8). Candidates qualify independently.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:74:Per arm and replicate: receiver survival, receiver-labelled biomass inventory, flux attribution (synthesis share; "growth" only at share at least half and no saturated counter), builder cost, before/after inventories. Ledger invariant per arm: lattice + reserve + cumulative heatAnalytic against that arm's own starting total, exact `0n` at every census of both stages, including every replicate (the unchanged rung-1 audit; no credit term). Evidence adds per arm: free E, total energy, `constructionGap` vs `b`.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:79:- `far-field-energy` (new, 2.1: fools rung-1 `D_inoc`, not `D_inoc2`; full gate rejects via `D_poly = 0`); also a uniform and an in-footprint variant, `caughtBy` recorded.
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:82:- Guards re-run: overwrite, pool cap, underfunded reserve, exhausted reserve, plus `credit-overdraw` (a free-matched comparator that would need negative lattice E or exceed a cap is refused, not clamped).
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:87:Block seeds in `8,601,001-8,601,999` and every derived replicate seed are reserved in `experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json` (owner `i06b free-energy-matched`) **before** anything runs. Replicate sims use block seed + `k * 1000`, `k = 0..7` (R = 4 uses k = 0..3; R = 8 uses k = 0..7), so derived seeds reach 8,608,999: the reservation lists the contiguous range `8,601,001-8,608,999`; the implementer first runs `seedCollisions` over it and, if any existing owner collides, replaces the offset by a collision-free stride and records that in the amendment. The protocol hash binds the explicit enumerated list of every seed that can run (block, replicate, noise, wall, fixture) and the registry digest; `--check` rescans the enumerated list, not only base lists. Never reused: 8,600,101-108, 8,600,501-508, nor any other rung-1 seed. Dev blocks 8,601,001-8,601,008, dev replicate-noise 8,601,201-8,601,204, wall 8,601,301-8,601,308, fixtures 8,601,401-8,601,408, fresh 8,601,501-8,601,516. Dev outcomes may set only `T1, T2` (unchanged unless dev shows cause), `delta_c`, the dose rungs and ceilings, and `R` (4 or 8; one escalation to 8 allowed if the noise gate fails, recorded before the freeze). Fresh seeds are read once, after the freeze commit is pushed and verified (section 9).
docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:93:Rung-1 rules with these changes only: 16 blocks; `inoc2` replaces `inoc`; per-contrast exact one-sided sign test (zeros non-positive) with `median >= delta`; field arm viable in at least 14 of 16 blocks (viability requirement, no survival-benefit claim); candidate p = max over four; Holm across candidates that ran. Adequacy is per candidate: the ordinary-placement positive control qualified that candidate at a dose within its ceiling and G-NOISE held (4.3); the rung-1 polymer criterion is a reported field only. `unsupported-within-tested-domain` only when valid, adequately measured and a gating test fails; its meaning is bounded by the power statement in 4.2.      1	// Exact, audited transfers between the conditioning stage and the assay stage. Every operation is analytical (outside the step
     2	// function), integer, and checked against the budget invariant: lattice + off-lattice reserve + cumulative analytic heat.
     3	//   energy:  lattice.energy + heatOut + heatAnalytic + reserveEnergy  ==  energyStart + lightIn
     4	//   matter:  lattice.matter + reserveB                                ==  matterStart
     5	// `ledgerResidual` counts neither the reserve nor heatAnalytic, so the wrapper below is the audited quantity.
     6	import { CELL_CHANNELS, CH, G, GENOME_CHANNELS, MOT_ZERO, POOL_MAX, cellCount, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldConfig, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     7	
     8	export class NicheInvalid extends Error {
     9	  reason: string;
    10	  constructor(reason: string) {
    11	    super(reason);
    12	    this.reason = reason;
    13	  }
    14	}
    15	
    16	export interface Audit {
    17	  energyStart: bigint;
    18	  matterStart: bigint;
    19	  /** Receiver reserve still off the lattice: B valued at eB per quantum, E at one. */
    20	  reserveB: number;
    21	  reserveE: number;
    22	  /** Cumulative energy released by analytical conversions, booked as heat. */
    23	  heatAnalytic: bigint;
    24	}
    25	
    26	export const reserveEnergy = (cfg: WorldConfig, a: Audit): bigint => BigInt(cfg.eB) * BigInt(a.reserveB) + BigInt(a.reserveE);
    27	
    28	export function residuals(s: WorldState, a: Audit): { energy: bigint; matter: bigint } {
    29	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
    30	  return {
    31	    energy: t.energy + s.heatOut + a.heatAnalytic + reserveEnergy(s.cfg, a) - a.energyStart - s.lightIn,
    32	    matter: t.matter + BigInt(a.reserveB) - a.matterStart,
    33	  };
    34	}
    35	
    36	export function assertAudit(s: WorldState, a: Audit, where: string): void {
    37	  const r = residuals(s, a);
    38	  if (r.energy !== 0n || r.matter !== 0n) throw new NicheInvalid(`${where}: nonzero residual (energy ${r.energy}, matter ${r.matter})`);
    39	}

    41	export function cloneState(s: WorldState, cfg: WorldConfig = s.cfg): WorldState {
    42	  return { cfg, step: s.step, cells: s.cells.slice(), genome: s.genome.slice(), lightIn: s.lightIn, heatOut: s.heatOut, flux: s.flux.slice() };
    43	}
    44	export const cloneAudit = (a: Audit): Audit => ({ ...a });

    46	export interface Snap {
    47	  A: string;
    48	  B: string;
    49	  C: string;
    50	  P: string;
    51	  E: string;
    52	  S: string;
    53	  matter: string;
    54	  energy: string;
    55	  reserveB: number;
    56	  reserveE: number;
    57	  heatAnalytic: string;
    58	  living: number;
    59	}
    60	export function livingCount(s: WorldState): number {
    61	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    62	  let k = 0;
    63	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if ((s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0) k++;
    64	  return k;
    65	}
    66	export function snap(s: WorldState, a: Audit): Snap {
    67	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
    68	  return { A: `${t.A}`, B: `${t.B}`, C: `${t.C}`, P: `${t.P}`, E: `${t.E}`, S: `${t.S}`, matter: `${t.matter}`, energy: `${t.energy}`, reserveB: a.reserveB, reserveE: a.reserveE, heatAnalytic: `${a.heatAnalytic}`, living: livingCount(s) };
    69	}
    70	
    71	export interface TransferRecord {
    72	  op: string;
    73	  before: Snap;
    74	  after: Snap;
    75	  detail: Record<string, number | string>;
    76	  /** Both exactly "0" for a valid record. */
    77	  residual: string;
    78	  matterResidual: string;
    79	}
    80	
    81	/** Run one mutation of (s, a) and record before/after; a nonzero residual or a failed validateState throws. */
    82	export function audited(op: string, s: WorldState, a: Audit, fn: () => Record<string, number | string>): TransferRecord {
    83	  assertAudit(s, a, `${op} (before)`);
    84	  const before = snap(s, a);
    85	  const detail = fn();
    86	  const errs = validateState(s);
    87	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`${op}: validateState: ${errs[0]}`);
    88	  const r = residuals(s, a);
    89	  if (r.energy !== 0n || r.matter !== 0n) throw new NicheInvalid(`${op}: nonzero residual (energy ${r.energy}, matter ${r.matter})`);
    90	  return { op, before, after: snap(s, a), detail, residual: `${r.energy}`, matterResidual: `${r.matter}` };
    91	}
    92	
    93	/** Split `total` evenly over `cells` (floor share, remainder to the first cells in order) and take it from channel `ch`. */
    94	export function debitEven(s: WorldState, ch: number, total: number, cells: readonly number[]): void {
    95	  if (total === 0) return;
    96	  const m = cells.length;
    97	  if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell can fund the reserve");
    98	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    99	  const base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
   100	  for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) {
   101	    const share = base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0);
   102	    if (s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]] < share) throw new NicheInvalid(`underfunded: cell ${cells[j]} holds ${s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]]} < ${share} of channel ${ch}`);
   103	  }
   104	  for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]] -= base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0);
   105	}
   106	
   107	/**
   108	 * Move `dB` quanta of lattice nutrient (matter) and `dEnergy` units of lattice free energy into the reserve. The reserve gains
   109	 * B = dB (valued eB each) and E = dEnergy - (eB - eA) * dB, so the funded reserve energy is exactly what left the lattice.
   110	 */
   111	export function moveToReserve(s: WorldState, a: Audit, dB: number, dEnergy: number, from: readonly number[]): void {
   112	  const { eA, eB } = s.cfg;
   113	  const e = dEnergy - (eB - eA) * dB;
   114	  if (e < 0) throw new NicheInvalid("reserve energy would be negative");
   115	  debitEven(s, CH.A, dB, from);
   116	  debitEven(s, CH.E, dEnergy, from);
   117	  a.reserveB += dB;
   118	  a.reserveE += e;
   119	}
   120	
   121	/** Start an audit for `s` as it is (nothing off the lattice yet). */
   122	export function openAudit(s: WorldState): Audit {
   123	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
   124	  return { energyStart: t.energy + s.heatOut - s.lightIn, matterStart: t.matter, reserveB: 0, reserveE: 0, heatAnalytic: 0n };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** Fund the reserve (Br, Er) from the lattice: A down by Br, E down by (eB - eA) * Br + Er, over `from`. */
   128	export function fundReserve(s: WorldState, a: Audit, Br: number, Er: number, from: readonly number[]): TransferRecord {
   129	  return audited("fund-reserve", s, a, () => {
   130	    moveToReserve(s, a, Br, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * Br + Er, from);
   131	    return { reserveB: Br, reserveE: Er, cells: from.length };
   132	  });
   133	}

   135	const livingAt = (s: WorldState, i: number): boolean => {
   136	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   137	  return (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0;
   138	};
   139	
   140	/** Builder removal: living cells become non-living (lineage and genome cleared); B, P, E, A, C, S stay in place. */
   141	export function relabelLiving(s: WorldState, a: Audit): TransferRecord {
   142	  return audited("relabel-living", s, a, () => {
   143	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   144	    let cells = 0, B = 0;
   145	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   146	      if (!livingAt(s, i)) continue;
   147	      cells++;
   148	      B += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
   149	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = 0;
   150	      s.cells[CH.MOT * n + i] = MOT_ZERO;
   190	      const old = s.cells.slice(ch * n, (ch + 1) * n);
   191	      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[ch * n + i] = old[p[i]];
   192	    }
   193	    return { channels: channels.join(",") };
   194	  });
   195	}
   196	
   197	/** A cell that may take receiver inoculum: nothing bound, no lineage, and room in E. `allowP` is for the hand-wall floor only. */
   198	export function placeable(s: WorldState, i: number, perE: number, allowP = false): boolean {
   199	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   200	  return s.cells[CH.B * n + i] === 0 && (allowP || s.cells[CH.P * n + i] === 0) && !livingAt(s, i) && s.cells[CH.E * n + i] + perE <= POOL_MAX;
   201	}
   202	
   203	/** Cells that are placeable in every state of the block (the intersection), in raster order. */
   204	export function commonCells(states: readonly WorldState[], disc: readonly number[], perE: number, allowP = false): number[] {
   205	  return disc.filter((i) => states.every((s) => placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)));
   206	}
   207	
   208	/** Move the whole reserve onto `cells` (even split, remainder to the first): empty cells only, nothing overwritten. */
   209	export function placeInoculum(s: WorldState, a: Audit, cells: readonly number[], words: Uint32Array, allowP = false): TransferRecord {
   210	  return audited("place-inoculum", s, a, () => {
   211	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   212	    const m = cells.length;
   213	    if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell to place the inoculum on");
   214	    if (a.reserveB === 0 && a.reserveE === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("reserve exhausted: nothing left to place");
   215	    const bBase = Math.floor(a.reserveB / m), bRem = a.reserveB % m;
   216	    const eBase = Math.floor(a.reserveE / m), eRem = a.reserveE % m;
   217	    const perE = eBase + (eRem > 0 ? 1 : 0);
   218	    for (const i of cells) if (!placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)) throw new NicheInvalid(`placement refused: cell ${i} is occupied or at its pool cap`);
   219	    const B = a.reserveB, E = a.reserveE;
   220	    cells.forEach((i, j) => {
   221	      s.cells[CH.B * n + i] += bBase + (j < bRem ? 1 : 0);
   222	      s.cells[CH.E * n + i] += eBase + (j < eRem ? 1 : 0);
   223	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = words[g];
   224	    });
   225	    a.reserveB = 0;
   226	    a.reserveE = 0;
   227	    return { placedCells: m, placedB: B, placedE: E };
   228	  });
   229	}

   231	export const CELLS_LEN = (cfg: WorldConfig): number => cellCount(cfg) * CELL_CHANNELS;
    20	  return { matterStart: BigInt(p.nutrient * n), energyStart: BigInt(p.backgroundE * n + 10 * p.reserve.B + p.reserve.E) };
    21	};

    23	const setChannel = (s: WorldState, ch: number, cells: readonly number[], total: number): void => {
    24	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), m = cells.length, base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
    25	  cells.forEach((i, j) => (s.cells[ch * n + i] += base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0)));
    26	};

    28	/** Run all pre-inoculum arms of a fixture block through the shared placement and Stage 2. */
    29	export function runPre(arms: PreArm[], p: Protocol, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>> {
    30	  placeAll(arms, p, opts);
    31	  const out: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>> = {};
    32	  for (const x of arms) out[x.id] = runArm(x, p);
    33	  return out;
    34	}

    36	/** Controls around a conditioned pre-arm `b` (any hand-built field): scrambles, ablation, receiver-alone and matched-inventory. */
    37	export function controlsAround(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: Protocol): PreArm[] {
    38	  const mk = (id: ArmId, ...ops: ((s: WorldState, a: Audit) => unknown)[]): PreArm => {
    39	    const state = cloneState(b.state), audit = cloneAudit(b.audit);
    40	    ops.forEach((f) => f(state, audit));
    41	    return { id, state, audit, records: [] };
    42	  };
    43	  const ALL = [CH.A, CH.B, CH.C, CH.P, CH.E, CH.S];
    44	  const sc = (chs: number[]) => (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => scrambleChannels(s, a, seed, chs, lowbias32);
    45	  const { d1E, d1B } = matchedControls(b, d0, p);
    46	  const nb = { id: "nb" as ArmId, state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
    47	  return [
    48	    { ...b, id: "b" },
    49	    mk("c", sc(ALL)),
    50	    mk("cP", sc([CH.P])),
    51	    mk("cNP", sc(ALL.filter((c) => c !== CH.P))),
    52	    mk("ab", (s, a) => convertPolymer(s, a)),
    53	    nb,
    54	    d0,
    55	    d1E,
    56	    d1B,
    57	  ];
    58	}

    60	/** Halo ring cells: in the footprint-plus-halo region but not in the footprint. */
    61	export const regionCellsExcludingFootprint = (p: Protocol): number[] => {
    62	  const fp = new Set(footprintCells(p));
    63	  return regionCells(p).filter((i) => !fp.has(i));
    64	};

    69	export type GiftVariant = "uniform" | "footprint" | "annulus";
    70	/** Extra stored free energy per variant, sized so the naive metric clears the fixture delta in each. */
    71	export const GIFT: Record<GiftVariant, number> = { uniform: 10000, footprint: 4000, annulus: 4000 };
    72	
    73	/** A world that differs from receiver-alone only by extra stored free energy, declared in its budget (it is a different budget on purpose). */
    74	export function giftBlock(variant: GiftVariant, seed: number, p: Protocol = fixtureProtocol(), gift = GIFT[variant]): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts; gift: number } {
    75	  const cfgSeed = seed;
    76	  const budget = uniformBudget(p);
    77	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: budget }, cfgSeed, p);
    78	  const cond: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
    79	  const W = p.grid;
    80	  const cells = variant === "uniform" ? Array.from({ length: W * W }, (_, i) => i) : variant === "footprint" ? footprintCells(p) : regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
    81	  setChannel(cond.state, CH.E, cells, gift);
    82	  cond.audit.energyStart += BigInt(gift);
    83	  const arms = controlsAround(cond, base, seed, p);
    84	  const outcomes = runPre(arms, p);
    85	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOf(outcomes), gift };
    86	}

    88	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89	// hand-wall
    90	
    91	export type WallKind = "W1" | "W2";
    92	
    93	/** W1: a polymer ring around the footprint (gates A/C diffusion). W2: a polymer floor under the footprint cells (a local-P shield under injuryShell). */
    94	export function wallCells(kind: WallKind, p: Protocol): number[] {
    95	  const f = p.footprint;
    96	  if (kind === "W2") return footprintCells(p);
    97	  const inner = new Set(disc(f.x, f.y, f.radius + p.wall.ringGap - 1, p.grid, p.grid));
    98	  return disc(f.x, f.y, f.radius + p.wall.ringGap + 1, p.grid, p.grid).filter((i) => !inner.has(i));
    99	}
