   101	/** Build P on `cells` out of lattice nutrient on the same cells and pay (eP - eA) * P from lattice E outside the region. */
   102	export function buildWall(x: PreArm, cells: readonly number[], totalP: number, p: Protocol): void {
   103	  audited("build-wall", x.state, x.audit, () => {
   104	    const s = x.state;
   105	    debitEven(s, CH.A, totalP, cells);
     1	// Pure helpers for the second-rung section of the niche page (free-energy-matched comparators, noise vs effect, dose ladder, edge per
     2	// candidate, far-field-energy panel). Reads the sealed second-rung evidence only and runs no simulation.
     3	import { edgeOf, esc, fmt, half, ratText, type Rational } from './render.ts';
     4	
     5	interface FreeRowView { id: string; freeE: string; total: string; constructionGap: string; flag: string }
     6	interface BlockFeView { seed: number; valid?: boolean; freeBefore?: FreeRowView[]; freeAfter?: FreeRowView[]; invalidReason?: string | null }
     7	interface DoseStat { k: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number }
     8	interface DoseRowView { totalP: number; seeds: readonly number[]; stats: Record<string, DoseStat>; limiting: string; limitingMedianTimes2: number }
     9	interface QualView { cluster: number; delta: number; ceiling: number; minDetectableDose: number | null; qualifyingDose: number | null; qualified: boolean; reason: string }
    10	interface ResultFeView {
    11	  id: string; label: string; simulated: boolean; mechanism: string; reasons: string[]; pCand: Rational; holmThreshold: Rational | null;
    12	  gating: Record<string, { k: number; n: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number; passes: boolean }>;
    13	  reported: { inoc: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number }; naive: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number } };
    14	}
    15	interface FixtureFeView { id: string; seeds: readonly number[]; naiveK: number; naiveWouldSay: string; inocFooledOld: boolean; inocCaughtNew: boolean; kOld: number; kNew: number; gatingK: Record<string, number>; caughtBy: string[]; fullGateLabel: string; freeBefore: FreeRowView[] }
    16	export interface EvidenceFeView {
    17	  protocolHash: string; integritySha256: string; amendments?: string[]; powerStatement: string; limits: string[];
    18	  protocol: { R: number; freshSeeds: readonly number[]; wallSeeds: readonly number[] };
    19	  dev: { R: number; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; deltaProposed: number; builderPMedianTimes2: number }[]; ladder: { rows: DoseRowView[]; w2: { status: string; reason: string } }; qualification: QualView[] };
    20	  fresh: { ran: boolean; seeds: readonly number[]; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>; survivalPositiveBlocks: number; input: { viableBlocks: number; expectedBlocks: number } }[]; results: ResultFeView[]; panel: { verdict: string; text: string }; caveats: string[] };
    21	  fixtures: { reports: FixtureFeView[]; freeGap: { seed: number; d0FreeE: string; bFreeE: string; d0Total: string; bTotal: string }[]; guards: { id: string; ok: boolean }[] };
    22	}
    23	
    24	export const COMPARATOR_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
    25	  b: 'Builder removed (the field arm)',
    26	  d0: 'Receiver alone, total energy matched',
    27	  d1E: 'Receiver alone + field energy near the inoculum',
    28	  d1B: 'Receiver alone + field matter near the inoculum',
    29	  fU: 'Free-energy matched, spread uniformly',
    30	  fS: 'Free-energy matched, same pattern cell for cell',
    31	  fB: 'Free-energy matched + the field\'s biomass',
    32	};
    33	const FREE_ORDER = ['b', 'd0', 'd1E', 'd1B', 'fU', 'fS', 'fB'];
    34	const GAP_WORD: Record<string, string> = { 'comparator-deficit': 'holds less total energy than the field', 'comparator-excess': 'holds more total energy than the field', 'total-matched': 'total energy equal', reference: 'reference' };
    35	
    36	/** Free E and total energy per comparator for one block: the d0-versus-b gap (the 9,917 vs 22,169 style numbers) and what matching does to it. */
    37	export function freeEnergyHtml(rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined, seed: number | undefined): string {
    38	  if (!rows?.length) return '<tr><td colspan="4">No block carries a free-energy table.</td></tr>';
    39	  const by = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
    40	  return FREE_ORDER.filter((id) => by.has(id)).map((id) => {
    41	    const r = by.get(id)!;
    42	    return `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(COMPARATOR_TEXT[id] ?? id)}</th><td>${fmt(Number(r.freeE))}</td><td>${fmt(Number(r.total))}</td><td>${r.id === 'b' ? 'reference' : `${esc(fmt(Number(r.constructionGap)))}: ${esc(GAP_WORD[r.flag] ?? r.flag)}`}</td></tr>`;
    43	  }).join('') + (seed === undefined ? '' : `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">Block seed ${seed}, before the inoculum is placed. Gap = total(field arm) minus total(comparator).</td></tr>`);
    44	}
    45	
    46	export function noiseEffectHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    47	  const rows = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => {
    48	    const row = ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.qualifyingDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.minDetectableDose);
    49	    const effect = row ? row.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2 : null;
    50	    const max = Math.max(1, q.delta, effect ?? 0);
    51	    const bar = (v: number | null, cls: string) => `<span class="${cls}" style="width:${Math.round((100 * Math.max(0, v ?? 0)) / max)}%"></span>`;
    52	    return `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.delta)}</td><td>${effect === null ? 'n/a' : half(row!.limitingMedianTimes2)}${row ? ` at dose ${fmt(row.totalP)} (${esc(row.limiting)})` : ''}</td>
    53	      <td><div class="bar" role="img" aria-label="noise floor delta ${fmt(q.delta)} against the wall effect ${effect === null ? 'not detected' : fmt(effect)}">${bar(q.delta, 'bar-noise')}</div><div class="bar">${bar(effect, 'bar-effect')}</div></td></tr>`;
    54	  });
    55	  return rows.join('');
    56	}
    57	
    58	export function doseLadderHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    59	  const n = ev.protocol.wallSeeds.length;
    60	  const rows = ev.dev.ladder.rows.map((r) => {
    61	    const gk = Object.entries(r.stats).map(([k, s]) => `${esc(k)} ${s.k}/${n}`).join(', ');
    62	    const passes = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `c${q.cluster}: ${r.totalP <= q.ceiling ? 'within ceiling' : 'above ceiling'}`).join('; ');
    63	    return `<tr><th scope="row">W1 ring, polymer ${fmt(r.totalP)}</th><td>${gk}</td><td>${esc(r.limiting)} ${half(r.limitingMedianTimes2)}</td><td>${esc(passes)}</td></tr>`;
    64	  });
    65	  rows.push(`<tr><th scope="row">W2 floor under the receiver</th><td colspan="3">${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.status)}: ${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.reason)}</td></tr>`);
    66	  return rows.join('');
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function qualificationHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    70	  return ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.ceiling)}</td><td>${q.minDetectableDose === null ? 'none on the ladder' : fmt(q.minDetectableDose)}</td><td>${q.qualified ? `qualified at ${fmt(q.qualifyingDose)}` : 'not qualified'}</td><td>${esc(q.reason)}</td></tr>`).join('');
    71	}
    72	
    73	export function edgeFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    74	  return ev.fresh.results.map((r) => {
    75	    const e = edgeOf(r.label, r.mechanism);
    76	    const g = ['builder', 'poly', 'space', 'inoc2'].map((k) => (r.simulated ? `${esc(k)} ${r.gating[k].k}/${r.gating[k].n}${r.gating[k].passes ? '' : ' (no)'}` : `${esc(k)} not run`)).join(', ');
    77	    const word = r.simulated ? e.text : 'Not simulated: the positive control did not qualify this builder, so there is no negative and no positive here.';
    78	    return `<li class="edge-fe edge-${e.state}"><strong>${esc(r.id.replace('cluster-', 'Builder cluster '))}: edge ${esc(e.word)}.</strong> ${esc(word)}<br><span class="muted">Label <code>${esc(r.label)}</code>${r.reasons.length ? ` (${esc(r.reasons.join('; '))})` : ''}. Blocks positive: ${g}.</span></li>`;
    79	  }).join('');
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function farFieldHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    83	  const rows = ev.fixtures.reports.map((f) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)}</th><td>${esc(f.naiveWouldSay)} (${f.naiveK} of ${f.seeds.length})</td><td>${f.kOld} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocFooledOld ? 'fooled' : 'not fooled'}</td><td>${f.kNew} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocCaughtNew ? 'caught' : 'not caught'}</td><td>${esc(f.fullGateLabel)}${f.caughtBy.length ? ` (rejected by ${esc(f.caughtBy.join(', '))})` : ''}</td></tr>`);
    84	  return rows.join('');
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function verdictFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    88	  const f = ev.fresh;
    89	  const stopped = f.ran ? '' : '<p class="verdict-stop"><strong>Stopped before the fresh-seed run.</strong> No fresh block was simulated, so there are no per-candidate contrasts: this states how sensitive the assay is at realistic polymer doses, not that no niche is built.</p>';
    90	  return `<p class="verdict-text"><strong>${esc(f.panel.verdict.replaceAll('-', ' '))}.</strong> ${esc(f.panel.text)}</p>${stopped}
    91	    <ul>${f.caveats.map((c) => `<li>${esc(c)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
    92	    <p class="muted">Protocol hash <code>${esc(ev.protocolHash.slice(0, 16))}</code>, seal <code>${esc(ev.integritySha256.slice(0, 16))}</code>. Each arm summed over ${ev.protocol.R} replicate sims; ${f.ran ? `${f.seeds.length} fresh blocks per qualified candidate` : 'no fresh blocks'}; ${ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks.length ?? 0} dev blocks per candidate were used for calibration only.</p>`;
    93	}

    95	export function pendingHtml(): string {
    96	  return '<p class="muted">The second-rung evidence has not been written yet (docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/evidence.json). Run <code>node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --write</code> after the freeze.</p>';
    97	}

    30	export function compactBlock(b: BlockResult): CompactBlock {
    31	  return {
    32	    seed: b.seed,
    33	    cluster: b.cluster,

    35	    invalidReason: b.invalidReason,
     1	// I06 edge decision: exact integer sign tests, intersection-union within a candidate, Holm across candidates, and the
     2	// three-valued panel verdict. Everything is an integer or a rational {num, den} of integers (no floats), so the sealed
     3	// payload canonicalizes. The rules are those of docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/PLAN.md section 5.3.
     4	import type { EvidenceStatus } from "@bl/schema";
     5	
     6	export interface Rat {
     7	  num: number;
     8	  den: number;
     9	}
    10	
    11	/** a <= b for rationals with positive denominators (all values here are far below 2^53 / each other). */
    12	export const ratLe = (a: Rat, b: Rat): boolean => a.num * b.den <= b.num * a.den;
    13	export const ratMax = (rs: Rat[]): Rat => rs.reduce((m, r) => (ratLe(m, r) ? r : m));
    14	
    15	const choose = (n: number, k: number): number => {
    16	  let c = 1;
    17	  for (let i = 1; i <= k; i++) c = (c * (n - k + i)) / i;
    18	  return Math.round(c);
    19	};
    20	
    21	/** One-sided exact sign test: P(Binomial(n, 1/2) >= k), where k counts strictly positive paired differences (zeros are non-positive). */
    22	export function signTestP(n: number, k: number): Rat {
    23	  let num = 0;
    24	  for (let j = Math.max(0, k); j <= n; j++) num += choose(n, j);
    25	  return { num, den: 2 ** n };
    26	}
    27	
    28	export const positives = (d: readonly number[]): number => d.filter((x) => x > 0).length;
    29	
    30	/** Sum of the two middle values of the sorted differences (n even) or twice the middle one (n odd): median >= delta iff this >= 2*delta. */
    31	export function medianTimes2(d: readonly number[]): number {
    32	  const s = [...d].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    33	  const n = s.length;
    34	  return n % 2 === 1 ? 2 * s[(n - 1) / 2] : s[n / 2 - 1] + s[n / 2];
    35	}
    36	
    37	export interface ContrastResult {
    38	  n: number;
    39	  k: number;
    40	  p: Rat;
    41	  medianTimes2: number;
    42	  /** p <= alpha and median >= delta. */
    43	  passes: boolean;
    44	}
    45	
    46	export function contrastResult(d: readonly number[], delta: number, alpha: Rat): ContrastResult {
    47	  const k = positives(d);
    48	  const p = signTestP(d.length, k);
    49	  const m2 = medianTimes2(d);
    50	  return { n: d.length, k, p, medianTimes2: m2, passes: ratLe(p, alpha) && m2 >= 2 * delta };
    51	}
    52	
    53	export const GATING = ["builder", "poly", "space", "inoc"] as const;
    54	export type Gating = (typeof GATING)[number];
    55	export const ALPHA: Rat = { num: 1, den: 20 };
    56	
    57	export interface CandidateInput {
    58	  id: string;
    59	  status: "ok" | "invalid" | "not-run";
    60	  invalidReasons: string[];
    61	  /** Per-block paired differences on the fresh blocks, one array per gating contrast, plus the attribution-only ones. */
    62	  contrasts: Record<Gating, number[]> & { spaceP: number[]; spaceNP: number[] };
    63	  delta: number;
    64	  survivedBlocks: number;
    65	  blocks: number;
    66	  /** The builder history produced polymer on dev (above the replicate floor). */
    67	  polymerAdequate: boolean;
    68	  /** Blocks assigned to the candidate; all must be valid. */
    69	  expectedBlocks: number;
    70	}
    71	
    72	export interface CandidateResult {
    73	  id: string;
    74	  label: EvidenceStatus | "not-run";
    75	  mechanism: "polymer-arrangement-attributed" | "non-polymer field pattern" | "combined conditioned field" | "n/a";
    76	  reasons: string[];
    77	  gating: Record<Gating, ContrastResult>;
    78	  attribution: { spaceP: ContrastResult; spaceNP: ContrastResult };
    79	  pCand: Rat;
    80	  holmThreshold: Rat | null;
    81	  holmRejected: boolean;
    82	  survivalPass: boolean;
    83	}
    84	
    85	export function decideCandidates(cands: readonly CandidateInput[], positiveControlPassed: boolean, minSurvived = 7): CandidateResult[] {
    86	  const ran = cands.filter((c) => c.status !== "not-run");
    87	  const m = ran.length;
    88	  const alphaEach: Rat = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * Math.max(1, m) };
    89	  const results = new Map<string, CandidateResult>();
    90	  const raw = (d: readonly number[], delta: number): ContrastResult => contrastResult(d, delta, { num: 0, den: 1 });
    91	  for (const c of cands) {
    92	    const g = Object.fromEntries(GATING.map((k) => [k, raw(c.contrasts[k], c.delta)])) as Record<Gating, ContrastResult>;
    93	    results.set(c.id, {
    94	      id: c.id,
    95	      label: "not-run",
    96	      mechanism: "n/a",
    97	      reasons: [],
    98	      gating: g,
    99	      attribution: { spaceP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceP, c.delta, alphaEach), spaceNP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceNP, c.delta, alphaEach) },
   100	      pCand: c.status === "ok" && c.blocks === c.expectedBlocks ? ratMax(GATING.map((k) => g[k].p)) : { num: 1, den: 1 },
   101	      holmThreshold: null,
   102	      holmRejected: false,
   103	      survivalPass: c.survivedBlocks >= minSurvived,
   104	    });
   105	  }
   106	  // Holm step-down over the candidates that ran (an invalid one counts with p = 1, never dropped).
   107	  const order = ran.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!).sort((a, b) => a.pCand.num * b.pCand.den - b.pCand.num * a.pCand.den || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1));
   108	  let stopped = false;
   109	  order.forEach((r, i) => {
   110	    r.holmThreshold = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * (m - i) };
   111	    if (!stopped && ratLe(r.pCand, r.holmThreshold)) r.holmRejected = true;
   112	    else stopped = true;
   113	  });
   114	  // A gating contrast passes when its own p is within the threshold the candidate faced in Holm and its median clears delta.
   115	  for (const c of ran) {

   138	export type ProtocolFe = typeof PROTOCOL_FE;
   139	
   140	/** Every seed that can run in this rung: block, replicate (primary and noise-control offsets), wall, fixture. Sorted, unique. */
   141	export function enumerateSeedsFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): number[] {
   142	  const out = new Set<number>();
   143	  const offs = (n: number, s: number) => {
   144	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) out.add(s + k * p.replicateStride);
   145	  };
   146	  for (const s of [...p.devSeeds, ...p.freshSeeds, ...p.wallSeeds]) offs(p.R, s);
   147	  for (const s of p.fixtureSeeds) offs(p.fixtureR, s);
   148	  for (const s of p.devReplicateSeeds) offs(2 * p.R, s);
   149	  return [...out].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   150	}
   151	
   152	/** SHA-256 of the canonical second-rung protocol, the registry digest it was checked against, and the enumerated seed list. */
   153	export function protocolHashFe(registryDigest: string, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): string {
   154	  return sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON({ protocol: p, registryDigest, seeds: enumerateSeedsFe(p) })));
   155	}

codex
The stopped outcome is justified, but the freeze enforcement needs fixes before acceptance.

1. **P1 — The guard does not verify the actual remote.** [tools/niche/fe.ts:96](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/tools/niche/fe.ts:96) reads `origin/...`, a local remote-tracking ref. A stale or locally updated ref can satisfy the check without the remote currently containing that freeze. This affects both guarded CLI modes. **Fix:** query the remote branch, obtain its commit OID, and compare against that exact fetched tree; refuse when remote verification fails.

2. **P1 — The exported generator bypasses both guards.** [tools/niche/fe.ts:140](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/tools/niche/fe.ts:140) exports `generateFe()` without calling `guarded()`. With a qualifying calibration, calling it directly reaches fresh simulations without verifying the lock, cleanliness, or push. The current all-stopped calibration avoids that execution, but the claimed invariant is unenforced. **Fix:** guard the exported orchestration entry point itself and test direct invocation against invalid and unpublished freezes.

3. **P2 — PLAN and Amendment 3 are outside the enforced freeze.** [tools/niche/fe.ts:78](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/tools/niche/fe.ts:78) includes sources, registry, calibration, and lock, but omits `PLAN.md`. Its amendment can change without invalidating the lock or guard, contrary to section 9. **Fix:** hash PLAN in the lock and include it in `frozenPaths`; test that a document-only amendment change causes refusal.

4. **P2 — The frozen interpretation of a negative overclaims.** [packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts:136](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts:136) converts failure to detect into “no benefit … in … about 90% of blocks.” The table describes one sign test on `D > 0`; it does not establish an exclusion bound for `D >= delta`, four simultaneous contrasts, or viability. Moreover, when only one candidate runs, the first Holm rejection needs 12 positives, not 13. **Fix:** describe negatives as benefits not detected under these rules, qualify the table’s candidate count and scope, and remove the exclusion claim from PLAN and payload limits.

5. **P2 — Ladder stopping can prevent an otherwise qualifying dose from being tested.** [packages/runner/src/niche/dose.ts:133](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/packages/runner/src/niche/dose.ts:133) stops once every candidate has a *wall-passing* dose, ignoring G-NOISE. Reproduced counterexample: delta 60, ceiling 170, dose 60 effect 100 passes the wall but fails noise; dose 120 effect 200 qualifies, yet would never run. **Fix:** continue through eligible doses until full qualification is established or eligible doses are exhausted; track minimum detectable dose separately. This does **not** change the present result: every recorded dose fails the wall rule.

6. **P3 — fB is incorrectly described as necessarily having excess total energy.** [packages/runner/src/niche/freeEnergy.ts:75](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/packages/runner/src/niche/freeEnergy.ts:75) and PLAN §4.1 assert `gap < 0`. The construction adds energy relative to **fS**, while its gap relative to **b** remains positive in dev: cluster 0’s first block has `constructionGap = +10380`. The test named for negative gap only checks that fB’s gap is smaller than fS’s. **Fix:** distinguish these reference points and make documentation and tests assert the actual signed relationship.

7. **P3 — The “did not rescue a benefit” comparison is structurally predetermined.** [PLAN.md:162](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:162) interprets negative `inoc2` as evidence about free-energy matching. Since its maximum retains both old comparators, `inoc2 <= inoc` necessarily; d1B wins that maximum in all 24 dev blocks. **Fix:** retain the registered gate, but report matched-only attribution separately. Those stored results are negative in 21 blocks and positive in three.

8. **P3 — Amendment 3’s numerical summaries need correction.** [PLAN.md:156](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md:156) reports a 15–35% rescaled floor reduction; the listed numbers give approximately **15.5%, 11.4%, and 27.7%**. Section 10 also calls approximately 30 the best limiting effect; **29.5 is the space median**, while the best limiting median is **1.5**. **Fix:** derive these summaries directly from calibration values.

Verified: the lock matches all **38 sources**, calibration, reservation digest, and **176 enumerated seeds**. Saved sums, contrasts, deltas, ceilings, and wall summaries agree; W2 reproduces zero placeable cells. The stopped report returns three `unsupported-measurement` labels, zero simulated candidates, and an empty negative list; the page explicitly distinguishes this from a negative.

Free-E matching, signed-gap arithmetic, shared placement, and audited conservation appear correct. One existing dev block replayed identically. Physics packages, golden pins, `RULE_VERSION`, and `defaultConfig` are unchanged; the original protocol is an unchanged prefix, `arms.ts` changes only its type, and rung-1 `--check` passes. Full replay and the test suite were not run.

No files changed; no fresh seeds were simulated.
